## Review

**6 out of 10, Needs substantial work.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck has a consistent identity (cyan palette, concentric-circle motif, footer bar) and a clear problem-to-solution run on slides 11 and 12, with big readable type and strong photography. The route is weakened by front-loaded and back-loaded legal text (slides 2 and 43 are tiny, unreadable walls of text), SPAC and presenter slides before the company is introduced, and a close on risk factors and a photo with no summary or next step. Many mid-deck slides are small-type and hard to read: slides 5 and 31 have tiny captions, slides 8 and 40 have very small text, slides 18 and 22 have barely legible bubble labels, and slides 23 to 27 repeat a template with small bullets over low-contrast screenshots. Slide 34 charts are clean but the bars are unevenly scaled and the layout is busy, so the strongest content is still diluted by density and uneven polish.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the visual identity, the problem and solution pair on slides 11 and 12, the cover, and the clear numbered tables on slide 41.

**Most useful repair:** Move disclaimers and risk text to an appendix in readable type, tighten the story order so the company case comes first, and enlarge the small text and labels on slides 8, 16, 18, 22 and 40.

**What would change the score:** Add an executive summary near the start and a closing slide that restates the case and the next step, and cut the repeated product slides down to key screenshots with large callouts.

_Confidence high. 44 of 44 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

This August 2024 investor presentation, prepared for the proposed business combination between Concord Acquisition Corp II and Events.com, Inc., opens with a title slide and then a long disclaimer covering forward-looking statements, the proposed transaction and securities-law notices. It introduces the SPAC team and its investing experience, then moves to the company: a category-defining domain name, key investment highlights and a brand-value slide built on domain purchase prices. The core company section describes the problem of fragmented event tools, the unified platform as the solution, company metrics and a management and investor roster. A product section walks through creator tools (sell, sponsor, promote, execute, insights) and the Discover consumer recommendation engine. Market sizing, business model and key metrics follow, then transaction overview and a dense risk-factor section. The deck closes on the risk factors rather than a call to action. Slides worth studying include the problem-to-solution sequence on slides 11 and 12, the market-size comparison on slide 34 and the sources-and-uses table on slide 41.

## Questions about this deck

**What market size does the Events.com investor presentation give for its opportunity?**

Slide 7 lists Events.com's key investment highlights, including a category defining domain name and a $1.8T TAM. The slide gives no further detail on how that figure was calculated.

**How many pages does the Events.com investor presentation have?**

The Events.com investor presentation has 44 pages. The title slide is slide 1, and the risk-factor section comes near the end of the deck.

**How does the Events.com presentation describe the problem and the solution?**

Slide 11 says event creators use multiple disparate systems that don't play well together, and event goers need a better way to discover events. Slide 12 presents the solution as a single, unified, multi-lingual, multi-currency platform for event creators and attendees.

**What creator tools does the Events.com presentation list in its product offerings?**

Slide 21 lists the creator tools as Sell, Sponsor, Promote, Execute and Insights. The same slide lists the event goer features as Explore, Find, Buy, Share and Belong.

Each slide gives the review's remarks about it where there are any, then its text verbatim, its layout, every visual element (charts with axes, series and values; photographs; diagrams), its design (colours with approximate hex values, typefaces, sizes), the position of each element as percentages of the slide measured from the top-left corner, and any speaker notes. Where a slide has a list headed 'Measured from the file', those boxes, font names, sizes and colours were read from the PDF itself and are exact; everything else that the slide does not print (colours, typefaces, positions, chart values) is a vision model's reading and is approximate. **Layout**

Full-bleed photograph covers the whole slide with the wordmark events.com placed in the upper-left third at about 9% from the left edge. The title Investor Presentation and the date line AUGUST 2024 are left-aligned in a block starting at about 34.7% from the left and sitting in the lower-middle area, with no footer, page number or slide number.

**Visuals**

One full-bleed photograph of a night scene: several people, seen from below, raise their arms toward a large glowing paper sky lantern in the upper centre-right, with its open flame visible at about 60% across and 35% down. Hundreds of smaller lit sky lanterns float across a black sky, and fine sparks drift off the large lantern. Lighting is warm orange and yellow from the lanterns against near-black; the composition is low-angle and framed to full width. A thin white concentric-ring graphic overlays the photo: about six circular rings of hairline white stroke, roughly 1 to 2 px wide at about 40 to 60% opacity, centred near 33% across and 50% down, with some rings extending off the slide edges. A white-filled rectangle at x 47.8%, y 26.9%, 6.7% by 7.9% sits in the photo and is not visually distinct. The events.com wordmark is a white geometric sans-serif logotype with a cyan (#1EB4D6) full stop between 'events' and 'com'.

Background: Full-bleed photograph of a night sky with floating lanterns, over a #0C3891 blue ground. Full-bleed photograph over a deep royal-blue ground (#0C3891) that is not visible. No corner radii or gradients on text; no shadows.

Additional Information about the Proposed Transaction and Where to Find It
In connection with the proposed business combination, the Form S-4 is expected to be filed by Concord with the SEC. The Form S-4 will include a preliminary proxy statement for the stockholders of Concord that will also constitute a preliminary prospectus. When available, the definitive proxy statement/prospectus will be distributed to holders of Concord’s common stock in connection with Concord’s solicitation for proxies for the vote by Concord’s stockholders in connection with the Proposed Transaction and other matters to be described in the Form S-4. Concord urges investors, stockholders and other interested persons to read, when available, the Form S-4, including the proxy statement/prospectus contained therein, as well as other documents filed with the SEC in connection with the Proposed Transaction, as these materials will contain important information about the Company, Concord, and the Proposed Transaction. Interested parties will also be able to obtain free copies of such documents filed with the SEC (once available) at the SEC’s website located at www.sec.gov, or security holders may direct a request to Concord Acquisition Corp II, Attn: Corporate Secretary, 477 Madison Avenue, 22nd Floor, New York, NY 10022.
Concord, the Company and their respective directors, executive officers and other members of their management and employees, under SEC rules, may be deemed to be participants in the solicitation of proxies of Concord’s security holders in connection with the Proposed Transaction. **Layout**

Two-column layout split by a thin vertical grey divider at about 64.5% of the slide width. The left block holds four presenters and a 4 by 3 logo grid under the heading 'Select Company Experience'. The right block holds two presenters and a 2 by 3 logo grid under the same heading. The title sits top-left at about 2% from the left edge. The events.com logo is centred over the left block and the Concord Acquisition logo sits top-right, above the right block. A full-width footer band runs along the bottom with the events.com wordmark at left and the page number at right. A solid circle sits in the top-right corner.

**Visuals**

Eight circular black-and-white headshot photographs, each cropped to a circle and set in a row of four on the left and a row of two on the right. Left to right: Mitch Thrower, a smiling man with light hair in an outdoor setting with trees and a light sky; Stephen Partridge, a man with glasses and a grey beard against a plain grey background; Bob Bellack, a smiling man in dark clothing against a black background; Paul Brown, a man in a light open-collared shirt against a grey background; Bob Diamond, a smiling man in a dark suit and tie against a dark grey background; Jeff Tuder, a bald man in a dark suit and tie against a light grey background. Each portrait is a head-and-shoulders crop, centred, with no overlay. Logo grid, left block, 4 columns by 3 rows, row 1: ACTIVE network. (Active Network wordmark in dark grey with a yellow-orange swoosh), EVENTS ONLINE CA (red and blue wordmark with a small figure), Apartments.com (green recycling-style icon and dark grey wordmark), WHEAT THE WHEAT GROUP, INC. CALIFORNIA, USA (black eagle emblem with serif wordmark). Row 2: competitorgroup (grey wordmark with blue swoosh), BIBNUMBERS.com (blue and black wordmark), cars.com (purple swoosh with purple wordmark), HYBRID APPAREL (dark grey wordmark). Row 3: triathlete MAGAZINE (black wordmark), START UP CANADA (red wordmark with maple leaf), DRAGON | GLOBAL (blue wordmark with vertical bar), Villeroy & Boch 1748 (black crown emblem with serif wordmark). Logo grid, right block, 2 columns by 3 rows: BARCLAYS (blue eagle emblem above blue wordmark), FORTRESS (blue diamond-lattice emblem with dark grey wordmark), ATLAS MERCHANT CAPITAL (blue circular emblem above small spaced grey wordmark), JHL CAPITAL (dark serif wordmark), CREDIT SUISSE FIRST BOSTON (red CREDIT SUISSE over blue FIRST BOSTON), inseego (grey and blue wordmark). Logos are placed on the light background without boxes. Events.com logo: black lowercase wordmark 'events' and 'com' with a single cyan dot between them, centred over the left block. Concord Acquisition logo: a blue-and-navy rotated-square swirl mark above the spaced capitals CONCORD ACQUISITION in dark grey. Decorative elements: faint thin white-and-grey concentric arcs sweep from the top across the slide. One solid cyan circle in the top-right corner. Horizontal gradient rules (navy fading to light) run left and right of each 'Select Company Experience' heading.

**Design**

Background is a vertical gradient from pale cyan #BFF3FF at the top to white #FFFFFF around the middle and lower area. Title colour #1D1F20 near-black. Presenter names in black #000000 Poppins Bold. Titles beneath names in black #000000 Poppins Regular. Section headings in navy #0C3891 Poppins Bold. Accent cyan #00BCDA for the footer band, the top-right circle and the dot in the events.com logo. Footer and page number in white #FFFFFF. Typeface is Poppins throughout: geometric sans-serif. Title is Poppins Bold at about 5.2% of slide height (cap height about 3.6%). Names are Poppins Bold at about 2.2% of slide height. Role lines and logo labels are Poppins Regular at about 1.5% of slide height. Section headings are Poppins Bold at about 2.5% of slide height. Thin rules are 0.5% of slide height, navy gradient. Vertical divider is a light grey 1 px line. Decorative arcs are hairline white and grey strokes at low opacity. No shadows, no rounded cards, no gradient on the photographs.

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a 16:9 slide. Left column (about 1.5% to 67% of width) holds four rows, each with a centred line icon above or beside a bold heading on the left and bullets on the right, separated by dotted rules. Right column (about 72% to 98%) holds the Concord Acquisition logo at top, then two portrait photos each with a name, title and bulleted bio. The title sits top-left at about 2% left margin and 3.4% from top, left-aligned. A full-width cyan footer band runs along the bottom 6% with 'events.com' at left and page number 4 at bottom right. No body text is in the footer band apart from these.

**Visuals**

Four line icons in cyan #00BCDA, stroke about 2% of slide height. (1) Top-left, about 7.7% x 14% to 12.4% x 23%: a standing businessman silhouette with tie and a globe at its right side. (2) About 8% x 35% to 13% x 45%: a dollar sign at centre surrounded by eight small circular nodes connected by short lines, like a network. (3) About 7.4% x 55% to 13% x 64%: three people silhouettes with heads connected to a central node above, forming a network. (4) About 7% x 77% to 13% x 84%: a handshake with two cuffed arms clasping. Concord Acquisition logo at about 76.7% x 7.9%, 19.2% wide: a blue-and-navy ring of two interlocking curved swooshes forming a diamond-like loop, with the text CONCORD ACQUISITION beneath in widely letter-spaced dark grey capitals. Portrait of Bob Diamond at about 72.2% x 33%, 8.9% x 15.8%, circular crop: black-and-white head-and-shoulders photo of a smiling man with short hair, dark suit, white shirt and striped tie, plain grey background, soft even lighting, dark vignette at edges. Portrait of Jeff Tuder at about 72.4% x 65.9%, 8.8% x 15.5%, circular crop: greyscale head-and-shoulders photo of a bald man in a dark suit and patterned tie, neutral grey background, even frontal light. Decorative solid cyan circle top right at 92.8% x 1.5%, 5.5% x 9.8%. Cyan play triangles (#00BCDA) pointing right beside each name at about 81.6% x 35.8% and 81.6% x 65%, roughly 0.8% wide. Dotted rules in dark blue #1E4D8C, roughly 0.2% tall, spanning about 22.4% to 68.9% of width at about 32.2%, 52% and 73.3% of height. Background: faint white curved strokes sweep from top to right edge over the gradient, thin and semi-transparent.

**Design**

Background is a vertical gradient from pale cyan #BDF3FC at top to white #FFFFFF by about 60% height, with faint white curved strokes overlaid. Title is Poppins Bold (geometric sans-serif), dark charcoal #1D1F20, about 5.2% of slide height, sentence/title case. Section headings are Poppins Bold, #1D1F20, about 2.6% of slide height. Bullet body text is Poppins Regular, #1D1F20, about 2.5% of slide height. Bullet glyphs are small cyan #54D5E6 dots in Arial. Person names are Poppins Bold in cyan #00BCDA at about 2.2%; titles are Poppins Regular #1D1F20 at about 2.2%. Sub-bullets are Poppins Regular #1D1F20 at about 1.7%, with black Arial bullets. Accent colour is cyan #00BCDA for footer band, top-right circle, icons, names and play triangles. Footer text is Poppins Regular white #FFFFFF. Shapes are flat with no shadows, rounded corners or gradients.

### Slide 5

**Review notes**

- Review: Many mid-deck slides are small-type and hard to read: slides 5 and 31 have tiny captions, slides 8 and 40 have very small text, slides 18 and 22 have barely legible bubble labels, and slides 23 to 27 repeat a template with small bullets over low-contrast screenshots.

**Text**

Built to spark
human
connection
through shared
experiences
events.com
5

**Layout**

Two-column layout: a solid cyan panel fills the left 23.1% of the slide full height, holding a left-aligned text block vertically centred at about 37.6% to 63.4% of the height with a 1.5% left margin. The photograph fills the remaining right 77% edge to edge. The footer 'events.com' sits bottom-left over the cyan panel at about 1% from the left edge, and the slide number sits bottom-right over the photograph at about 98.4% x and 96.4% y. There is no separate title placeholder.

**Visuals**

One photograph and one logo. Photograph: a very large glossy, mirror-like blue sphere, a sculpture, dominates the centre of an urban plaza. Its surface reflects surrounding skyscrapers and the sky, shading from deep blue at upper left to bright light cyan at upper right. Its lower part reflects a dense crowd of people. Tall cream and beige stone skyscrapers with many windows stand at left and right, and green trees are at lower left. Crowds of people fill the foreground at the bottom edge, cropped by the slide edge. Blue sky with thin white wisps is at the top. Lighting is bright daylight from the upper right, producing a specular highlight on the sphere. Dominant colours are blue, cyan, white, cream and green. The photograph is full-bleed, not boxed, with no tint over the left panel. It is a wide framing with the sphere centred slightly right. Logo: the wordmark 'events.com' in white lowercase bold sans-serif, centred on the sphere at about 47.5% x and 43.7% y, with a cyan period between 'events' and 'com'.

**Design**

Background: Solid cyan #00BCDA panel on the left, with a photograph covering the whole slide behind it. Cyan #00BCDA left panel with a full-bleed photograph on the right. Titles and body text are white #FFFFFF in Poppins, a geometric sans-serif. The body statement is Poppins Regular, with the words 'shared experiences' in Poppins Bold. Size is about 4.4% of slide height for the statement and about 1.5% for the page number. The footer 'events.com' is bold white Poppins at about 3% of slide height. **Layout**

Left-aligned headline block sits vertically centred in the left half, starting at about 4.4% from the left edge. A right-hand column of three stacked circular photos runs down the right side, with a logo beside each photo, and the footer and slide number sit along the bottom edge.

**Visuals**

Three circular photographs stacked vertically in a column at about x 53%. Top: four young women walking across a plaza beneath a large glass-and-steel pyramid structure, one woman in the centre with her arm raised, warm daylight, muted grey and teal tones, circle crops the top edge of the slide. Middle: a pale pink-white sky lantern glowing with an open flame inside, lifted against a near-black night sky, with hands reaching up toward it and small white lights around, dark dominant colour, larger circle overlapping the top and bottom photos. Bottom: a crowd of people seen from behind with arms raised, one wearing a red sleeve, under a pale blue sky with many colourful hot-air balloons, circle crops the bottom edge of the slide. Logos: Wonderfront Music & Arts Festival wordmark in white stylised capitals with the words MUSIC & ARTS FESTIVAL beneath in small white caps; RiSE logo in dark blue letters on a white circle; Temecula Valley Balloon & Wine Festival logo in white and dark blue-white with a ribbon banner reading TEMECULA VALLEY above BALLOON & WINE FESTIVAL and EST. 1983 below. A small solid teal circle sits in the top-right corner.

**Design**

Background: Solid colour #2859BB, royal blue, covering the full slide. Solid royal blue background #2859BB with no gradient or texture. Headline text is white #FFFFFF in Poppins Regular, sans-serif geometric, about 5.9% of slide height per line with line spacing about 7.1% of height. The final phrase every event is Poppins Bold in cyan #00BCDA. Footer events.com is Poppins SemiBold in white, about 2.8% of height. Page number is Poppins Regular white, about 1.5% of height. Photos are circular crops with no border or shadow. Teal accent circle is a flat #00BCDA disc with no outline.

### Slide 7

Key Investment Highlights
events.com

7

**Layout**

Full-bleed slide with the title top-left at about 2% from the left edge, aligned to the top margin. Five highlight items sit along a curved blue arc on the left-centre, each with a round icon badge on the arc and its label to the right, stepping right then back left down the slide. The events.com logo sits at the left, vertically centred on the arc. A cyan footer bar runs full width at the bottom with the events.com wordmark left and the slide number right.

**Visuals**

Five circular navy icon badges placed on a curved navy arc, each containing a thin white line icon: top badge a stacked-layers icon; second badge a line chart rising arrow inside a square; third badge a shield with a check mark; fourth badge a target with an arrow; bottom badge a single person silhouette outline. The arc runs from a fading pale-blue stroke at the top-left to a fading stroke at the bottom-left. Behind the content on the right is a set of large concentric thin white-to-grey arc lines, partly transparent, sweeping from the top to the bottom right. A solid cyan circle sits at the top-right corner. The events.com logo is a dark charcoal lowercase wordmark with a cyan period.

**Design**

Background is a vertical gradient from pale cyan #BFF4FF at the top to white #FFFFFF from about the middle down. Title text is near-black #1D1F20 in Poppins Bold at about 5.2% of slide height, with capitals and lowercase in sentence-style title case. Highlight labels are black #000000 Poppins Bold at about 3.0% of slide height. Accents are cyan #00BCDA (footer bar, top-right circle, logo period) and navy #0D3A8F-style blue #0B3A8E for the arc and badges. Icons are white thin line glyphs. Badges are perfect circles with no shadow. The arc is a thick stroke about 2% of slide width in navy with fading gradient ends. Lines in the background rings are very thin, about 0.2% of slide width, in white with light grey edges. No corner radii; no shadows.

### Slide 8

**Review notes**

- Review: Many mid-deck slides are small-type and hard to read: slides 5 and 31 have tiny captions, slides 8 and 40 have very small text, slides 18 and 22 have barely legible bubble labels, and slides 23 to 27 repeat a template with small bullets over low-contrast screenshots.
- Suggested repair: Move disclaimers and risk text to an appendix in readable type, tighten the story order so the company case comes first, and enlarge the small text and labels on slides 8, 16, 18, 22 and 40.

**Text**

Category Defining Domain Name / SEAL OF THE SUPREME COURT OF THE UNITED STATES / DICTIONARY / .com / events.com / ● Trillions of dollars invested in the .com phenomenon / ● .com is the world's most popular top level domain and it carries elevated status and legitimacy / ● In June 2020, the Supreme Court allowed the trademarking of a generic term associated with a domain name(1) / ● Events is one of only approximately 171k words in “current use” in English(2), and one of only approxiamtely 1,025,109 words in the English Dictionary(3) / ● ”Owning the .com is like owning the word globally.” / ● Gannett acquired the Cars.com business at an enterprise valuation of $2.5B and valued the Cars.com domain name at $872M(4) / ● Like Hotels.com, people perceive Events.com as a marketplace for Event Organizers, Event Goers and Sponsors / ● The Brand enjoys significant “mindshare” before the company’s products & services have been exposed to the consumer / ● People naturally bestow incumbency status as a successful and large brand globally / Domain Name Purchase Price ($M)(5) / cars.com / Apartments.com / business.com / CarInsurance.com / insurance.com / VacationRentals.com / Fact of the HomeAway Family / PrivateJet.com / internet.com / The E-Business and Internet Technology Network / Hotels.com / $872M / 2014 / $585M(6) / 2014 / $350M / 2007 / $50M / 2010 / $36M / 2010 / $35M / 2007 / $30M / 2012 / $18M / 2009 / $11M / 2011 / (1) CNN (2) Word Counter (3) National Grammar Day (4) Smart Branding (5) Publicly available information (6) Refers to purchase price of entire Apartments.com entity / events.com / 8

**Layout**

Three-column layout above a full-width price strip: two vertical navy divider rules at about x 32 and x 66 split the upper area into three text columns, each headed by a logo or image (.com with seal, dictionary book, events.com), with the title across the top left and a page number at bottom right. Title sits top-left at about x 2 and y 3.4, left-aligned with a margin of about 2 percent; a footer bar runs full width along the bottom 6 percent with the events.com wordmark at left and page number 8 at right; footnotes sit in a single line above the footer.

**Visuals**

Full-slide light cyan gradient background with thin white concentric arcs in the upper-right half. Top-centre-left: a black-and-white .com wordmark (blue-cyan dot) and a circular grey-and-black Seal of the Supreme Court of the United States with an eagle, shield and US flag, boxed as a circle, no crop. Top-centre: a photograph-style illustration of a black hardcover DICTIONARY book standing at an angle with gold page edges, lit from the upper left, plain background. Top-right: events.com wordmark in black with a cyan dot instead of the o-dot. Price strip: a horizontal gradient rule from navy to white (a bar fading at its centre) at y about 72 percent, with the heading Domain Name Purchase Price ($M) centred on it. Below, nine logos in one row, each in its own colour: cars.com (purple oval with white script), Apartments.com (green leaf mark and dark grey text), business.com (grey and tan wordmark), CarInsurance.com (dark blue car icon with blue text), insurance.com (bold navy text), VacationRentals.com (orange and blue text, with small 'Fact of the HomeAway Family' line), PrivateJet.com (dark grey text), internet.com (black box with white text and red accent), Hotels.com (red H mark and red text). Beneath each logo a price in bold and a year in regular: $872M 2014, $585M(6) 2014, $350M 2007, $50M 2010, $36M 2010, $35M 2007, $30M 2012, $18M 2009, $11M 2011. No charts, tables or photographs of people.

**Design**

Background: Light cyan gradient, #BFF3FF at top fading to white #FFFFFF at the middle and lower areas, with thin white concentric arc lines at the upper right.

### Slide 9

**Text**

Transaction Overview
Business Combination
Concord Acquisition Corp II (“Concord II”, “CNDA”) intends to complete a business combination
with Events.com, Inc. (the “Business Combination”)
The Business Combination is targeted to close in Q1 2025, subject to the satisfaction of
customary closing conditions
Valuation Overview
The Business Combination implies a pro forma enterprise value of approximately $399M(1)
Existing Events.com shareholders will roll 100% of their equity as part of the Business
Combination and own approximately 72% of the pro forma company
Capital Structure
SPAC Sponsor intends to help the Company raise up to $50M through a mix of cash in trust
and PIPE financing
(1)
Assumes a $434M pro forma equity value at closing, based on 43.4M shares outstanding, and $35M of pro forma net cash
events.com
9

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a full-bleed slide: a left column (about x 5% to 35%) holds the events.com wordmark stacked above the Concord Acquisition logo, both centred on roughly x 20%; a right column starting at x 40% holds three section headings, each with a thin blue rule beneath, and their bullet text. The title Transaction Overview sits top-left at x 2%, y 3.4%, with its left edge on the 2% margin. The footnote sits bottom-left at y 91.6%, and a full-width cyan footer bar occupies y 94% to 100% with the events.com wordmark at left and page number 9 at right.

**Visuals**

Two logos and decorative linework. (1) events.com wordmark, about 28% of slide width, dark charcoal lettering in a bold sans-serif, with a cyan dot in place of the period between events and com; it sits on a transparent background in the left column. (2) Concord Acquisition logo, about 25.5% of slide width: a circular blue emblem made of two interlocking curved bands forming a rounded diamond with an open centre, shaded from light steel blue to dark navy, above the wordmark CONCORD ACQUISITION in widely letter-spaced dark grey capitals. (3) Decorative background: about eight thin pale grey-white concentric arc lines sweeping from the top edge down and to the right across the right half of the slide, low opacity, passing behind the text column. (4) A solid cyan circle at top-right and a solid cyan footer bar. No photographs or charts.

**Design**

Background: Vertical gradient from light cyan #BFF3FF at top to white #FFFFFF at bottom, with thin white arc linework over the right side. Pale cyan-to-white vertical gradient background running from light cyan #BFF3FF at top to white #FFFFFF by about 60% height. Titles and headings in near-black #1D1F20 Poppins Bold, with the heading Capital Structure in #000000. Body text in #1D1F20 Poppins Light. Accents are cyan #00BCDA for bullet dots, the top-right circle, the footer bar and the events.com dot. Section rules are thin 1px-equivalent blue lines #3D4FA8. Title is about 5.2% of slide height, section headings about 3.0%, body about 2.0%, footnote about 1.2%, footer page number about 1.5%. Geometric sans-serif (Poppins family) throughout. Rules are straight, no corner radii, no shadows.

### Slide 10

**Text**

Company Overview

**Layout**

Single centred composition on a full-bleed photograph. The title sits in the lower-middle area, centred horizontally (about x 27 to 73 percent) at roughly 70 to 78 percent of the slide height, with the cyan dot and rings forming a focal point above it. There is no footer, slide number or logo, and no visible margins because the image covers the whole slide.

**Visuals**

1) Full-bleed photograph covering the entire slide: a close-up of two hands held in a heart shape in front of a bright, warm backlight. The hands are dark silhouettes with rings and bracelets on the wrists. Behind the heart is a glowing yellow-orange sun flare with small sparkles, and the left side shows a blurred cool blue-white light streak and bokeh. Lighting is strong backlight from the centre, dominant colours are amber, gold, orange and pale blue-grey, and the image is slightly desaturated and softened. The photo is full-bleed, not cropped to a box, with a faint grey-white haze overlay over the whole frame. 2) Concentric thin white outline rings, about eight to nine circles of stroke width roughly 0.2 percent of slide height, semi-transparent white at about 70 percent opacity, centred on the heart (around x 50 percent, y 37 percent), spreading past the slide edges and passing over the photo and the title. 3) A solid cyan filled circle at the centre of the rings, placed over the heart gap, with no outline and fill about #00BCD9, supplied as a 213 by 213 pixel image.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed photograph of hands forming a heart against a warm golden backlight, with a white #FFFFFF base fill beneath it. Full-bleed photographic background with a warm amber and gold colour grade and a light white haze. The title is set in Poppins Bold, a geometric sans-serif, in near-black #1D1F20, mixed case, not capitalised, about 7.9 percent of slide height, with normal tracking. The only accent is the cyan circle #00BCD9. The ring overlay is thin white at about 70 percent opacity with no shadows, gradients or corner radii.

### Slide 11

**Review notes**

- Review: The deck has a consistent identity (cyan palette, concentric-circle motif, footer bar) and a clear problem-to-solution run on slides 11 and 12, with big readable type and strong photography.
- Worth keeping: Keep the visual identity, the problem and solution pair on slides 11 and 12, the cover, and the clear numbered tables on slide 41.

**Text**

The Problem
Event creators globally are using
multiple disparate systems that
don’t play well together

Event goers need a better way to
discover and connect with the right
events for them
11
events.com

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a full-bleed slide. The circular photograph sits in the left column, from about 4.5% to 40% of the width and from about 6% to 69% of the height, with its top edge near the top margin. The text column starts at x 45.7% and holds the title at y 21% above two body paragraphs separated by one blank line; all text is left-aligned to a shared left edge. A full-width cyan footer bar occupies the bottom 6% of the slide, with the wordmark events.com at the left margin and the slide number 11 at the far right inside the bar.

**Visuals**

1. Photograph in a circle (about 35.4% by 62.9% of the slide, cropped to a circle): a young woman with shoulder-length dark brown hair and a striped light blue and white button-up shirt sits indoors, frowning with her right hand resting against her cheek while she looks down at a black smartphone held in her left hand. The grey and beige interior is softly blurred behind her; cool, diffuse daylight from the left; the composition is a head-and-shoulders crop with the face in the upper-middle of the circle. A soft grey drop shadow falls below and to the right of the circle.
2. Lime starburst (about 11.4% by 20.2% of the slide, placed at x 28.5%, y 49.1%): a radial burst of about 24 tapered rays of lime green #C4E80A radiating from a solid centre, overlapping the lower-right edge of the photograph.
3. Solid cyan circle (about 5.5% by 9.8% of the slide, top-right corner at x 92.8%, y 1.5%): flat fill #00BCDA, no outline.
4. Concentric arcs (decorative, right side of slide): a set of thin white strokes forming large concentric circular arcs that bulge from the top edge and sweep down across the right half of the slide, passing behind the body text; stroke appears white at about 1 pt on the pale cyan background.
5. Footer bar: flat #00BCDA rectangle across the full width at the bottom 6% of the slide.

**Design**

Background is a vertical gradient from pale cyan #BDF3FF at the top to white #FFFFFF by about the middle of the slide, then white to the footer. Title text is bold geometric sans-serif (Poppins Bold) in cyan #00BCDA, about 7.9% of slide height for the cap-height line. Body text is Poppins Regular in near-black #1D1F20 at about 4.4% of slide height, with key words set in Poppins Bold cyan #00BCDA (creators, goers). Accents are cyan #00BCDA and lime #C4E80A. The photo has a circular crop with a soft drop shadow; the starburst and cyan dot are flat with no shadows. Footer wordmark is white bold geometric sans-serif (Poppins SemiBold style) at about 3.5% of slide height; the page number is white Poppins at about 1.5% of slide height. Line spacing for body text is generous, about 1.6 times the font size.

### Slide 12

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A single-sentence solution slide that pairs the platform name with a plain benefit statement, following directly from the problem slide before it.
- Review: The deck has a consistent identity (cyan palette, concentric-circle motif, footer bar) and a clear problem-to-solution run on slides 11 and 12, with big readable type and strong photography.
- Worth keeping: Keep the visual identity, the problem and solution pair on slides 11 and 12, the cover, and the clear numbered tables on slide 41.

**Text**

The Solution
A single, unified, multi-lingual, / multi-currency platform that / helps event creators save time / and make more money and / helps attendees connect with / more events they love:
Events.com
events.com
12

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a 16:9 slide. A circular photograph with overlapping cyan circles sits in the left column, roughly x 4.5 to 41 percent. The title and body text are left-aligned in the right column starting at x 45.7 percent, with the title at about y 21 percent and the body block below it. A full-width cyan footer bar runs along the bottom 6 percent, with the events.com wordmark at bottom left and the page number 12 at bottom right. No header and no subtitle.

**Visuals**

1) Circular photograph, x 4.8 y 6.4 w 35.4 h 62.9 percent, cropped to a circle with a soft drop shadow falling to the lower right. Subject: a young woman with long reddish-brown hair in a loose windblown style, wearing a light blue and white vertically striped button-up shirt and a white smartwatch on her right wrist. She holds a pale pink or rose-gold laptop open in her left hand and holds a black smartphone to her right ear with her right hand. She looks off to the left with a serious expression. Setting: an outdoor event or stage area with out-of-focus metal truss or scaffolding on the left, a dark equipment case, and a blurred grey backdrop sign with partial Cyrillic letters. Lighting: bright natural daylight, slightly warm. Dominant colours: pale blue and white of the shirt, pink laptop, beige or yellow floor, grey and dark metal. Framing: medium shot, subject placed slightly right of centre within the circle, head near the top third. Not full-bleed. 2) Solid cyan circle, x 18.4 y 62.3 w 15.2 h 27.9 percent (rendered as an ellipse-shaped circle), fill #00BCDA, fully opaque, overlapping the bottom of the photograph. 3) Second solid circle, x 28.6 y 57.4 w 12.2 h 22.6 percent, fill #00BCDA at 84 percent opacity, placed behind the first cyan circle and overlapping the bottom right of the photograph. 4) Solid cyan circle at the top right, x 92.8 y 1.5 w 5.5 h 9.8 percent, fill #00BCDA, flat with no outline (the file lists this fill twice at the same position). 5) Decorative line art: a set of thin white concentric arcs, roughly 1337 by 1334 pixels, positioned on the right side of the slide (x 45.4 to 100 percent, y 0 to 96.9 percent), drawn as faint white lines with light grey edges that sweep from upper left to lower right over the light background and body text. 6) Solid cyan bottom bar, x 0 y 94 w 100 h 6 percent, fill #00BCDA, flat, no outline.

**Design**

Background is a soft vertical gradient from pale cyan at the top to white in the lower half, with white concentric arc lines on the right. Title colour is cyan #00BCDA. Body text colour is near-black #1D1F20. Accent colour is cyan #00BCDA, used for the title, the call-to-action link Events.com, the shapes and the footer bar. Typeface is Poppins, a geometric sans-serif: Bold for the title and the Events.com line and the footer wordmark, Regular for the body. Title is about 7.9 percent of slide height. Body text is about 4.4 percent of slide height with generous line spacing of roughly 6.9 percent of slide height per line. Shapes are flat circles with no outlines and no corner radii. Only the photograph has a soft drop shadow. No text outlines, no gradient on shapes.

### Slide 13

**Text**

WONDERFRONT / MUSIC FESTIVAL / 136,000 / Attendees(1) / The all-in-one platform / designed to manage / the full event cycle / operating globally in / multiple currencies and / languages / (1) Cumulative attendees for Wonderfront music festival / events.com / 13

**Layout**

Two-column split. A solid cyan panel fills the left 40.3% of the slide full height, holding a left-aligned statement block at x 3.2% that sits in the vertical middle, with a footnote and the events.com logo along the bottom-left. A full-height crowd photograph fills the right 59.8% edge to edge. A rounded cyan callout sits in the top-right of the photo, and the page number sits bottom-right on the photo.

**Visuals**

One photograph: a large outdoor music festival crowd seen from behind, facing a lit stage at night. In the foreground, two people in branded hoodies (one pink and white with the partial word 'wonder' printed on it, one yellow and white with 'nder' visible) are shown from behind. Below and beside them, a dense crowd of heads and shoulders fills the lower half, including a person in a white and grey striped knit beanie in the lower centre and a person in a grey cap to the right. The stage behind has a dark truss rig, a black banner reading 'events.com' across the top, blue LED bar lights and a white-hot spotlight near centre, a large rear projection screen showing a band performing, and side LED screens at the left and right edges. Lighting is night-time stage lighting: saturated blue and magenta light, a warm orange-red spot, and hazy atmosphere. Dominant colours are deep blue, violet, magenta and dark grey, with warm orange highlights. Framing is a wide-to-medium shot, cropped by the frame on all sides, full-height and bleeding off the top, bottom and right edges, with a hard vertical edge against the cyan panel on the left. No overlay tint is visible apart from the callout box. Also one rounded cyan callout box (see elements).

**Design**

Background: Solid cyan #00BCDA on the left 40.3% of the slide, with a full-bleed crowd photograph covering the right side. Flat cyan panel with a full-bleed photograph. Background colour cyan #00BCDA. Statement text, callout text, footnote and logo are white #FFFFFF; the callout box is cyan #00BCDA with white text, and the bold phrase 'operating globally' is white bold. Typeface is Poppins throughout: Regular for the statement and page number, Bold for the callout and the bold phrase, Light for the footnote, and a bold geometric sans for the logo. The statement text is about 5.2% of slide height (cap height about 3.6%), and the callout text is about 3.0% of slide height. The footnote is about 1.2% and the page number about 1.5%. Line spacing for the statement is generous, about 8% of slide height per line. The callout is a rounded rectangle with a corner radius of roughly 3% of slide height, with no border and no shadow. No gradients.

### Slide 14

**Text**

Events.com Positioning / events.com / Similar to / Business Model / salesforce / airbnb / Iconic Domain / cars.com / Apartments.com / Marketplaces / airbnb / Uber / fiverr. / shopify / MarTech / sprinklr / HubSpot / sproutsocial / salesforce / Ticketing / eventim / LIVE NATION / ENTERTAINMENT / vividseats / eventbrite / events.com / 14

**Layout**

A bold title sits top-left at about 2% from the left edge and 3.4% from the top. A large light-blue circle sits top-centre as a hub, with three outlined circles in a row across the lower half, one under each of the left, centre and right thirds. Elbow connectors run from the hub's bottom to each lower circle's top. A full-width cyan footer bar along the bottom holds the events.com wordmark at left and the slide number at right. A decorative cyan dot sits top-right and concentric arcs fill the right side.

**Visuals**

Diagram: one hub circle, filled pale periwinkle #D0DDF5, centred at about x 50%, y 26%, diameter about 27% of slide width. Inside it are the events.com wordmark, the 'Similar to' heading with thin gradient rules on either side, two labelled groups (Business Model: salesforce cloud logo and airbnb logo; Iconic Domain: cars.com oval logo and Apartments.com logo). Three outlined circles below, each with a centred heading and two logos in a 2x2 arrangement: left circle Marketplaces (stroke #7398E1, about 3 px) with airbnb, Uber, fiverr., shopify; centre circle MarTech (stroke #0C3891, about 3 px) with sprinklr, HubSpot, sproutsocial, salesforce; right circle Ticketing (stroke #00BCDA, about 3 px) with eventim (with a yellow four-point sparkle), LIVE NATION, vividseats, eventbrite. Connectors: a single vertical stem from the hub bottom at x about 50% splits into a horizontal bus at y about 51% that runs to the left and right; the left branch is periwinkle #7398E1 with a downward arrowhead onto the Marketplaces circle, the centre branch is navy #0C3891 with a downward arrowhead onto MarTech, and the right branch is cyan #00BCDA with a downward arrowhead onto Ticketing. Each connector is about 2 px thick. Short gradient underline rules sit beneath each lower circle's heading, coloured to match their circle. Logos are flat brand marks: airbnb coral #FF5A5F, Uber black, fiverr. green #1DBF73, shopify green #95BF47 with black wordmark, sprinklr multicolour, HubSpot dark grey with orange sprocket, sprout social dark with green leaf, salesforce blue #00A1E0 cloud, eventim navy #1D2F6F, LIVE NATION red #E01F26 box with black text, vividseats navy with magenta and teal triangle, eventbrite orange-red #F05537, cars.com purple oval #5B2D90 with white italic text, Apartments.com dark serif text with green recycle-style symbol #6CB33F. Decorative: a solid cyan circle top-right and a set of thin pale grey concentric arcs on the right half. No photographs.

**Design**

Background is a vertical gradient from pale cyan #BFF3FF at the top to near white #FFFFFF around the middle and lower area. Titles and headings are bold geometric sans-serif (Poppins Bold), dark #1D1F20 for the slide title and hub subheads, periwinkle #7398E1 for the Similar to heading and Marketplaces heading, navy #0C3891 for MarTech, and cyan #00BCDA for Ticketing and accents. Body and logo text is Poppins or similar geometric sans. Title is about 5.2% of slide height; sub-headings about 3.2%; small labels about 2.2%. Shapes are flat with no shadows. Circles have no corner radius, connectors are straight with square arrowheads, and the footer bar is solid cyan #00BCDA with no shadow.

**Design**

Background is a soft vertical gradient from light cyan #C0F4FF at the top to near-white #FFFFFF at the bottom. Title is Poppins Bold in near-black #1D1F20. **Layout**

Three-part vertical structure: a bold left-aligned title at the top left, a centred two-line subtitle beneath it, and seven equal columns of portrait, name, role and bullet list below, each column separated by thin light-grey vertical rules. The title sits at x 2% and the body columns start at about x 2%, with margins of about 2% on each side. A full-width cyan footer bar at the bottom carries the events.com wordmark at the left and the slide number at the right. A cyan circle sits at the top right corner.

**Visuals**

Seven circular headshot photographs, one per team member, each about 7.7% of slide width, in a row at the top of the columns. Mitch Thrower: smiling man with short light hair, outdoors with blurred trees behind, warm daylight, cropped to a circle. Stephen Partridge: bearded man in dark-rimmed glasses and dark top against a bright, pale background, cropped to a circle. Bob Bellack: smiling man with short hair in dark jacket against a black background, cropped to a circle. Paul Brown: man with short grey hair in light blue collared shirt against a grey background, cropped to a circle. Jeff Cameron: smiling man with short light-brown hair in dark suit and striped tie against a blue background, cropped to a circle. Cami Winding: woman with long wavy brown hair in light beige blazer against a grey background, cropped to a circle. Paolo Privitera: man with short dark hair in dark jacket and white open-collar shirt against a dark background, cropped to a circle. All photos are full colour, evenly lit, head-and-shoulders framing. Decorative thin white concentric arcs in the top right area. A solid cyan circle at top right. Thin light-grey vertical divider lines between columns. Small cyan bullet dots before each list item.

**Design**

Background is a soft vertical gradient from light cyan at the top to white near the bottom. Title is set in heavy geometric sans-serif (Poppins Bold), colour near-black #1D1F20, approximately 5.2% of slide height. Subtitle is Poppins Bold in deep blue #0C3891, about 3.0% of slide height, centred. Names are Poppins Regular in cyan #00BCDA, about 2.0% of slide height. Roles are Poppins Bold in dark slate #263238, about 1.7% of slide height. Bullet body text is Poppins Regular in near-black #1D1F20, about 1.7% of slide height, with bold used for company and product names. Accent colour is cyan #00BCDA for names, bullets, footer bar and the top-right circle. Footer bar is solid cyan #00BCDA with the wordmark in white bold lowercase and the page number in white regular. Divider lines are thin light-grey #D0D5DA, about 0.2% of slide width. Circle photos have no border or shadow. Arcs are thin white strokes at low opacity.

### Slide 17

**Text**

Supported by Network of High-Impact Investors and Advisors / Bill Trzos / CEO & Managing Partner, / Cypress Ascendant / Eric Schmidt / Former Chairman, Google / (Alphabet) / Dr. Bernice King / CEO, Martin Luther King Jr. / Center for Nonviolence / Harry Copperman / CEO, HDC Ventures / Fmr. Board of Directors, AOL / Doug Harrison / CEO, The Rise Club / Fmr. President U.S., YouGov / Alex Helm / VP, Gabelli Partners / CEO, Acumen Capital / Marco Landi / Fmr. COO & President, Apple / Jonathan Spalter / CEO, U.S. Telecom / Chair, Mobile Future / Assoc. & CIO, U.S. Information Agency / Tony Hawk / Founder, Tony Hawk Inc. / Calvin Johnson / Hall of Fame Wide Receiver, NFL / Pamela Moellenhoff / Early Investor, Salesforce / VP, Merrill Lynch / Giacomo Marini / President & CEO, / Noventi Ventures / Founder, Logitech / Conrad Riggs / Fmr. Head of Television Business, / Amazon / Billy Gerber / Fmr. President, Warner Bros / Chairman, Gerber Pictures / Victoria Lennox / President & CEO, / Lennox Innovations / Co-Founder, Startup Canada / Terry Moore / Managing Partner & Founder, / Moore Venture Partners and / the MVP Funds / John Hoegger / Fmr. Ex Machine Learning, / Microsoft / Raj Doshi / Pres. & COO, April / Fmr. Head of Strategy, Google Americas / events.com / 17

**Layout**

Title runs across the top left at about 3.6% from the top with a margin of about 2% from the left. Eighteen circular portraits sit in a three-row by six-column grid, each portrait followed by a centred two-line caption block. A full-width cyan footer bar occupies the bottom 6% with the events.com wordmark at left and the slide number 17 at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Eighteen circular headshot photographs in a 6 by 3 grid, each about 9.8% wide and 17.4% high, cropped to circles. Row 1 (left to right): Bill Trzos, a smiling man with short grey hair in a navy jacket against a white background; Eric Schmidt, a smiling man with glasses in a navy blazer with green foliage behind; Dr. Bernice King, a woman with dark hair in a dark jacket against a dark background; Harry Copperman, a smiling man in a striped polo shirt against a mustard-gold background; Doug Harrison, an older smiling man in a light blue shirt against a pale background; Alex Helm, a smiling man in a dark suit with a light blue striped tie against a yellow background. Row 2: Marco Landi, a smiling man in a bright blue jacket and white shirt against a white background; Jonathan Spalter, a smiling man in a grey shirt against a blurred brown background; Tony Hawk, a man with a black top against a solid black background; Calvin Johnson, a man in a blue NFL Lions jersey with number 81 against a blue stadium background; Pamela Moellenhoff, a smiling woman with auburn hair against a grey-blue background; Giacomo Marini, a man with grey hair in a dark suit against a dark background. Row 3: Conrad Riggs, a man with glasses against a dark grey background; Billy Gerber, a man in a dark suit and tie against a warm brown background; Victoria Lennox, a smiling woman with long blonde hair against a light grey background; Terry Moore, an older man with grey hair and a moustache in a blue shirt against a grey background; John Hoegger, a young man smiling broadly against a warm brown background; Raj Doshi, a smiling man against a dark warm background. All portraits are head-and-shoulders, centred, evenly lit, with natural colours. A faint white concentric curved line illustration covers the right half of the slide behind the photos. A solid cyan circle sits at the top right corner, and a solid cyan footer bar spans the bottom.

**Design**

Background: Vertical gradient from light cyan #BDF3FF at the top to white #FFFFFF at the bottom, with faint white curved lines on the right. Light cyan to white vertical gradient background. Title in Poppins Bold, near-black #1D1F20, about 4.7% of slide height for the cap-height line. Caption names in Poppins Bold, black #000000, about 2.2% of slide height. Affiliation lines in Poppins Regular, black #000000 with bold organisation names, about 1.5% of slide height. Accent colour cyan #00BCDA for the footer bar and top-right circle. Footer wordmark white Poppins SemiBold. Slide number white Poppins Regular. Portraits are circular crops with no border and no shadow.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

- shape at x 0, y 0, w 100, h 100 [#BDF3FF]: Background gradient, light cyan to white, vertical (Full-bleed vertical gradient from #BDF3FF at top to #FFFFFF at bottom.)

### Slide 18

**Review notes**

- Review: Many mid-deck slides are small-type and hard to read: slides 5 and 31 have tiny captions, slides 8 and 40 have very small text, slides 18 and 22 have barely legible bubble labels, and slides 23 to 27 repeat a template with small bullets over low-contrast screenshots.
- Suggested repair: Move disclaimers and risk text to an appendix in readable type, tighten the story order so the company case comes first, and enlarge the small text and labels on slides 8, 16, 18, 22 and 40.

**Text**

Venue
Box / Office
Paid / Media
Discovery
Analytics
Talent
Check In
Vendors
Ads
Event / Management
The event industry has multiple siloed solutions
Fragmented, costly and time consuming to manage
Emails
Virtual
Social / Media
Merchandising
Members
Ticketing
Sponsors
Marketing
Campaign
SEO
18

**Layout**

Full-bleed flat cyan slide with no visible grid. Scattered outlined circles of varied sizes fill the top band and bottom band, with a centred two-line headline block (bold title over a regular subtitle) in the middle band at about 45% to 56% of the slide height. The slide number sits in the bottom-right corner at about 2% margin from the right edge.

**Visuals**

Twenty-one thin white outlined circles, all unfilled, of varying diameters, each containing one centred white label. Circles are not connected by lines or arrows and do not overlap one another. Sizes range from about 5% to 17% of slide width. No photographs, logos, icons, charts or tables.

**Design**

Background: Solid cyan #00BCDA covering the whole slide. Flat cyan background #00BCDA with no gradient, texture or shadow. All text is white #FFFFFF. Title is Poppins Bold (geometric sans-serif) at about 5% of slide height, subtitle is Poppins Regular at about 3.5%, circle labels are Poppins Regular at about 2% and the page number is Poppins Regular at about 1.5%. Circles have white strokes about 1.5 px wide, no fill, no corner radius and no shadow. Circle labels are sentence case.

**Layout**

Full-bleed photograph fills the whole slide with no margins. A centred concentric-ring diagram sits in the upper-middle area around a central cyan disc, with the title centred at about 76% of slide height, the subtitle centred beneath it, and a small left-aligned note in the bottom-left corner. No visible slide number, footer or logo apart from the small trademark mark beside the disc.

**Visuals**

Diagram: a central solid cyan disc (about 10.7% width, 19% height of the slide) with a small ™ mark at its lower right, surrounded by four concentric thin white ring outlines (about 1 to 2 px, semi-transparent) centred on the disc. Twenty dark navy labels follow the arcs of the rings, rotated to follow each arc: on the outer arc Marketing (top), Paid Media, Promotional Tools, Venue, Virtual, Talent, Social Media, Merchandising, Check In, Analytics, Vendors and On-Site Apps; on the middle arc Ticketing, Membership, Event Management, Box Office, Campaign, Discovery and Data; on the inner arc sponsorship. Photograph: a wide view down a tree-lined city boulevard from an elevated viewpoint, with a dense crowd of runners in light shirts and dark shorts moving toward the viewer along the central lane, parked cars and stone buildings at the edges, and bright sky at the vanishing point. It is full-bleed, uncropped at the edges, with a strong cyan monochrome tint overlay (about #3FC3DC over the greyscale image) lightening toward the top and bottom. Dominant colours: cyan, pale blue, white and grey.

**Design**

Background is a full-bleed cyan-tinted photograph with concentric white ring outlines. Title is near-black #1D1F20 in Poppins Bold, sentence case, at about 7.9% of slide height, centred. Subtitle is #1D1F20 in Poppins Regular at about 3.5% of slide height, centred, with 'save time' and 'make more money' in Poppins Bold on the second line. Diagram labels are dark navy #13213F in Poppins Bold at roughly 2.3% of slide height. The central disc is solid cyan #00BCDC with no shadow. Ring outlines are thin white strokes at reduced opacity. Note is Poppins Light at about 1.2% of slide height in black #000000. No corner radii, gradients on shapes or shadows.

### Slide 20

**Text**

“The Airbnb and Salesforce of the Events Space”
Manage
Discover
Market
Interact
Monetize
Transact
Event Creators
(B2B)
Event Goers
(B2C)
A multi-sided marketplace powering the expansive event ecosystem
events.com
20

**Layout**

Free-form, not a grid: a bold title sits top-left at about 2% margin with a cyan circle top-right. Two circular photos sit left and right of a central white ring logo, with three left-aligned labels stacked on the far left and three right-aligned labels on the far right. White caption cards sit under each photo, a centred tagline sits near the bottom, and a full-width cyan footer bar carries the events.com wordmark at bottom-left and the page number at bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Two circular photographs, one on each side. Left photo: a young Black woman in a striped top and an orange head wrap, with a man in a grey-green shirt and striped top behind her, both looking at a clipboard and a laptop in a warehouse with red shelving and cardboard boxes; warm indoor lighting, cropped to a circle, shown mid-frame with the woman in the foreground. Right photo: a smiling woman with long brown hair in a bright red knit sweater dancing with one arm raised, with other party guests behind her in front of a colourful pink, blue and purple mural; daylight, bright saturated colours, cropped to a circle. Centre: a white circle with a navy #0C3891 outline containing the events.com logotype (dark grey text with a cyan full stop). Around the circles: thin arc arrows in periwinkle #7398E1 across the top and cyan #00BCDA arcs along the bottom, each ending in an arrowhead pointing toward the opposite side, forming a loop between the two photos and the centre. Six small navy line icons: a folder with cards (Manage), a megaphone with a hand and document (Market), a browser window with a dollar sign (Monetize), a magnifying glass (Discover), a phone with a hand tapping and signal waves (Interact), a coin with a hand pointer (Transact). Two white rounded caption cards with soft shadows sit under the photos.

**Design**

Background: Vertical gradient from pale cyan #BFF3FF at the top to white #FFFFFF by mid-slide, with faint white concentric arc lines at top right. Light cyan-to-white vertical gradient background with faint thin white concentric arc lines at the top right. Title in Poppins Bold, near black #1D1F20, about 5.2% of slide height. Labels in Poppins Bold navy #0C3891 at about 3.5% of height. Card captions in Poppins Regular navy #0C3891 at about 3.0% of height. Tagline in Poppins Bold navy #0C3891 at about 3.5% of height. Accent colours: cyan #00BCDA for the top-right circle, footer bar and bottom arcs; periwinkle #7398E1 for the top arcs. Geometric sans-serif throughout. Arc strokes are thin, about 2 px, with triangular arrowheads. Cards are white with soft grey drop shadows and small corner radii. Photos are circular crops with no border.

### Slide 21

**Text**

Events.com Product Offerings
Events.com is a single, unified software and service solution built to allow Event Creators to save time and generate more revenue and allow Event Goers to discover and transact with their favorite events
events.com
Event Creators (Create)
Event Goers (Discover)(1)
Sell
Management & sales
Sponsor
Sponsorship & brand activation
Promote
Marketing & promotion
Execute
Execution & onsite tools
Insights
Data & management tools
Explore
Event search and discovery
Find
Event match & recommendations
Buy
One-click register & transactions
Share
Social attendance
Belong
Find events with your friends
events.com
21
(1)
V2 planned to launch out of stealth in 2025

**Layout**

Title sits top-left at x 2 y 3.4 with left alignment and a roughly 2 percent margin, and the centred two-line subtitle sits beneath it at y 12.8 to 20. A large dashed cyan rounded frame spans about x 3.4 to 96.6 and y 23.4 to 89.2, holding a centred events.com logo at the top, then two columns: Event Creators on the left (header bar at x 4.5, with Sell, Sponsor and Promote in a left column and Execute and Insights in a middle column offset to the right) and Event Goers on the right (header bar at x 51.3, with Explore, Find and Buy in a column and Share and Belong in a further column offset to x 75). Footnote (1) sits bottom-left at y 91.6, a full-width cyan footer bar occupies the bottom 6 percent with a white events.com wordmark at left and page number 21 at right.

**Visuals**

Ten circular icons in two colour sets: five in the Event Creators column are pale cyan circles with cyan line icons, and five in the Event Goers column are solid navy circles with white line icons. Creators icons: Sell is an orbit diagram of three small circles on concentric rings; Sponsor is two overlapping circles; Promote is a broadcast signal of concentric arcs around a dot; Execute is a gear-shaped badge containing a checkmark; Insights is a line chart of connected nodes (a zigzag line with circular markers). Goers icons: Explore is a magnifying glass; Find is a target with an arrow; Buy is a ticket with a perforated edge; Share is two overlapping speech bubbles; Belong is an outlined heart. A small events.com logo (dark #1D1F20 wordmark with a cyan full stop) sits centred at the top of the frame, about 42 percent to 58 percent of width and 26.6 to 30.5 percent of height. A decorative set of faint concentric arc lines runs diagonally across the upper right of the slide. A solid cyan circle sits at the top right corner. The footer wordmark is white events.com text.

**Design**

Background is a pale vertical gradient from cyan #BFF3FD at the top to near white #F5FCFE at the middle and bottom. Title is near-black #1D1F20 in Poppins Bold, about 5.2 percent of slide height, all in sentence and title case. Subtitle body is Poppins Regular in deep blue #0C3891, about 3.0 percent of slide height, with key phrases in Poppins Bold. Column header text is white Poppins Bold about 3.0 percent. Item headings are Poppins Bold about 2.5 percent, in cyan #00BCDA for creators and navy #0C3891 for goers. Item descriptions are Poppins Regular in black #000000 about 2.2 percent. Accents are cyan #00BCDA and navy #003996. Header bars are flat rectangles with square corners, no shadows. The dashed frame is a cyan #00BCDA dashed stroke about 0.4 percent of slide height wide with small rounded corners. Icon circles have no shadows.

- Review: Many mid-deck slides are small-type and hard to read: slides 5 and 31 have tiny captions, slides 8 and 40 have very small text, slides 18 and 22 have barely legible bubble labels, and slides 23 to 27 repeat a template with small bullets over low-contrast screenshots.
- Suggested repair: Move disclaimers and risk text to an appendix in readable type, tighten the story order so the company case comes first, and enlarge the small text and labels on slides 8, 16, 18, 22 and 40.

**Text**

Events.com Sell
Sell more tickets, save more time, make more money
Key Highlights
● Build fully branded event ticketing pages
● Create multiple different ticket types with inventory / management and price changes
● Add custom email confirmations with QR codes by / product type and support multiple languages
● Add custom and conditional form questions with e- / sign capabilities
● Allow split payments for attendees
● Build robust % or $ off promotions and create multi- / quantity discounts
events.com Create
22

**Layout**

Single-slide content layout with a bold title at top left, a centred subtitle line across the top, and a left text column of five bulleted points below a centred section heading. A large screenshot composite sits on the right, with a laptop-style screenshot in front of a tall event-page screenshot. A full-width cyan footer band runs along the bottom edge with the brand wordmark at left and the page number at right; a cyan circle sits in the top-right corner. Left margin is about 2 percent; the bullet column starts at about 6.5 percent.

**Visuals**

Two screenshot mockups of an events.com ticketing page. Tall page (placed image at x 45.4, y 0, w 54.6, h 96.9, bleeds off the top and right edges): white card with a cropped photograph at top of cyclists riding in a blurred street, showing wheels and bike frames in warm orange, teal and grey tones; below it the title Cycling For Charity UK in dark grey, the line Saturday, July 9, 2022 07:00 AM - 04:00 PM GMT in small bold grey, and the word Blackburn in small grey; to its right a ticket panel partly hidden by the laptop with rows of grey 0 counters and round dark-grey plus buttons, and a pale salmon button at the bottom. Laptop mockup in front (x 53.1, y 45.0, w 35.6, h 44.7): black bezel with a white screen containing a photograph of three smiling people at a summer festival, one woman with curly hair and a man in a blue shirt raising arms, with blue sky and colourful party hats and a yellow-striped shirt; below it the title Summer Festival in dark grey, the line Friday, May 20, 2022 07:00 PM - 10:00 PM GMT in bold grey, North Greenwich Station in grey, and 6610 W Kellogg Dr, Wichita, KS 67209, USA in small grey. Below the title is a black TICKETS header bar with white text, then rows: Adult with a price of €130.00 EUR and Children with a price of €80.00 EUR, each with grey minus button, the counter 0, and a black round plus button; small grey disclaimer text reads By clicking 'GET TICKETS' you are agreeing to the Events.com Terms of Use and Privacy Policy with cyan links; a grey GET TICKETS button with a white 0 badge sits at bottom right. Laptop photo has bright, saturated natural daylight colours. Both mockups have soft drop shadows. Faint thin white concentric arc lines decorate the background behind them.

**Design**

Background is a vertical gradient from pale cyan at the top to near white at the middle and lower area, with faint thin white concentric arc lines sweeping across the upper right. Title Events.com in near black #1D1F20, Poppins Bold for the brand word and Poppins Regular for Sell; title is large at about 5.2 percent of slide height. Subtitle and section heading and bullets in dark royal blue #0C3891; subtitle Poppins Bold at about 3.5 percent of slide height; bullets and body Poppins Regular at about 2.5 percent. Bullets are solid dark blue dots, set in a Times-style glyph. A short horizontal rule under Key Highlights is a blue to white to blue gradient line. Accent colour is cyan #00BCDA for the footer band and the top-right circle. Footer wordmark events.com in white Poppins Bold with Create in white Poppins Regular. Page number in white Poppins at about 1.5 percent. Shapes are flat with no corner radius; screenshot mockups have soft shadows.

**Layout**

Single-column text block on the left with the title centred across the top and a centred Key Highlights subheading above a rule, bullets left-aligned; the right half holds a stacked cluster of product screenshots bleeding off the right edge. A full-width cyan footer bar carries the events.com Create wordmark at left and the page number 23 at right.

**Visuals**

Right-hand cluster of three product screenshots and one hand-drawn illustration, overlapping. (1) Back layer: a white sponsorship proposal document for RE/MAX Realty, shown as a flat page with a faint shadow, at about x 50.5 y 39.4 w 22 h 47.9; its contents include the title, a PPD Beach2Battleship logo at upper right, a Sponsor Contact block, an Event Overview paragraph, and a Participant Demographics bullet list (average household income $126,000, 98% have a 4-year college degree, 45% have a post-graduate degree, 81% 18-49 age demo, median age 38, 60% male). (2) Middle layer: a web form panel with two input fields, a list of branding options with checkboxes (several filled green), and a price list of sponsorship items on a white card, at about x 65.7 y 18.1 w 32.5 h 43.8; a curved grey arrow sweeps from the first option to the total, with a grey hand pointer illustration at about x 74.5 y 33.5 w 18.5 h 21; the total $17,087.50 is ringed by a green ellipse outline at about x 91.8 y 39.6 w 5 h 5.7. (3) Front layer: a dark charcoal events.com dashboard window at about x 62.4 y 54.4 w 34.5 h 31.3, with a left sidebar of cyan circular counters (14 Event Dashboard, 8 Proposals, 11 Agreements, 442 Prospective Sponsors) and a grid of eight event cards in cyan-titled tiles, each with date range, a small logo, and proposal and agreement counts. No photographs; no charts.

**Design**

Background: Full-slide light cyan-to-white vertical gradient (top about #C4F4FF, fading to #FFFFFF) with faint white arc lines. Light gradient background from pale cyan at top to white around the middle and lower area; thin white concentric arc lines run across the upper right. Title 'Events.com' in near-black #1D1F20 and 'Sponsor' in lighter weight of the same colour; subtitle and body in deep navy blue #0C3891; accent cyan #00BCDA for the top-right circle, footer bar and dashboard highlights. Typeface Poppins (geometric sans-serif); title about 5.2 percent of slide height bold, subtitle about 3.5 percent bold, body about 2.5 percent regular, footer text about 2.7 percent. Key Highlights rule is a thin gradient line navy to white to navy with a height under 1 percent. Screenshots have soft drop shadows and small corner radii; the green ellipse is a thin stroke. No gradients on shapes other than the rule.

Save time and money, reach a wider targeted audience / SE|N|Y / September 08, 2023 - New York / Rise NYFW / Your event on events.com / 1 Set up your campaign / Select days and locations for this campaign, additional campaigns can be added later / 4 Start your campaigns / Click Promote Now to proceed / Promotions start / Promotions end / Aug 28, 2023 / Sep 8, 2023 / Campaign duration: 12 days - From August 28, 2023 to September 08, 2023 / Estimated people reached / 51,429 - 64,287 / VERY HIGH / Events.com Network promotion / Events.com Browse: $ 60.00 / Calendar boost: $ 60.00 / Advertising channels / 100% ad budget is invested / Instagram Ads: $ 20.00 / Facebook Ads: $ 20.00 / Google Ads: $ 20.00 / Twitter Ads: $ 40.00 / LinkedIn Ads: $ 40.00 / Snapchat Ads: $ 20.00 / Pinterest Ads: $ 20.00 / Order subtotal: $ 300.00 / 10% service fee: $ 30.00 / Total USD 330.00 / Promote Now / Target key cities for this campaign / Add cities (max 15) / + Add / Powered by Google / New York / Remove / 2 Promote on Events.com and Partner Calendars / Target key regions and city calendars to reach millions of engaged and profiled event goers / Powered by Calendar Partners and events.com / Events.com Browse: Featured listing / Attract engaged users to your event, stand out on Events.com Browse and reach the top positions on Google Search results / $ 60 / More info

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a full-bleed slide: a page title sits top-left at the margin, a centred subtitle runs across the top third, the left column holds a Key Highlights heading with a four-item bullet list, and the right column holds a large app screenshot with three cyan callout labels overlapping its edges. A full-width cyan footer band runs along the bottom with the wordmark at left and the page number at the right margin.

**Visuals**

One screenshot of the Events.com Promote web interface, placed in a bordered white browser frame with a light grey top bar reading events.com and a promote sub-bar, boxed with a soft drop shadow, at about x 55.4 y 20.9 w 40.5 h 65.5 (a second copy of the same screenshot sits at x 56.0 y 21.7 w 39.3 h 63.3). It shows a campaign set-up page: a black square SE|N|Y logo with the event Rise NYFW, step 1 Set up your campaign with a date range Aug 28, 2023 to Sep 8, 2023, a Target key cities panel listing New York, step 2 Promote on Events.com and Partner Calendars with a Featured listing option at $ 60, and step 4 Start your campaigns with an estimated reach of 51,429 - 64,287 and a price breakdown totalling USD 330.00 with a dark blue Promote Now button. Three cyan curved arrows (fill #00BCDA, about 1 unit wide stroke) connect the callouts to the UI: from Suggested Campaign Timing down-left to the Promotions start date field, from Automated Targeting up to the Target key cities area, and from Optimized Campaign Spend up-right to the USD 330.00 total. Three cyan rounded-rectangle callout labels (#00BCDA, corner radius about 0.5 percent of slide height, soft shadow) carry white text. A thin horizontal divider under Key Highlights runs from dark blue to white to dark blue. No charts, tables or photographs.

**Design**

Background: Vertical gradient from light cyan #BDF4FF at top to white #FFFFFF around the middle, with faint white concentric arc lines overlay. Light cyan-to-white vertical gradient background with thin white concentric arc lines in the upper right; a solid cyan circle top right and a solid cyan footer band. Title Events.com in near-black #1D1F20 bold with Promote in regular weight; subtitle and body text in dark blue #0C3891; callouts cyan #00BCDA with white text; footer wordmark white. Typeface Poppins throughout (geometric sans-serif), bold for title, subtitle and heading, regular for bullets, light for callouts. Title about 5.2 percent of slide height, subtitle about 3.5 percent, Key Highlights about 2.5 percent, body about 2.5 percent, footer 2.7 percent. Bullets are solid dark blue dots, line spacing about 1.6 times the font size, with a blank line between items. Callouts are rounded rectangles with drop shadows.

### Slide 25

**Review notes**

- Review: Many mid-deck slides are small-type and hard to read: slides 5 and 31 have tiny captions, slides 8 and 40 have very small text, slides 18 and 22 have barely legible bubble labels, and slides 23 to 27 repeat a template with small bullets over low-contrast screenshots.

**Text**

Events.com Ad Exchange Media Network & Calendar
Fast, scalable reach from local to global
Key Highlights
● Powered event calendars on thousands of media and entertainment websites
● Monetizable marketing inventory
● Automated event ad impressions across Google, Facebook and the Events Network
● Recurring and scalable revenue
● High-quality lead generation for Events.com platform
events.com Create
25

**Layout**

Left-aligned title sits across the top at about 3 to 9 percent of slide height, with a centred subtitle beneath it at about 14 to 17 percent. A two-column split follows: the left column (about 5 to 48 percent width) holds a 'Key Highlights' heading over a rule and a bulleted list from about 37 to 75 percent height; the right column (about 45 to 100 percent width) holds a laptop mockup with a phone mockup overlapping its lower right. A full-width cyan footer band runs along the bottom 6 percent, carrying the wordmark at left and the page number at right. A cyan circle sits in the top-right corner, and the left margin is about 2 percent.

**Visuals**

Laptop mockup (x 45.4, y 0, w 54.6, h 96.9 percent, 1337 by 1334 pixels), a silver-grey laptop with a black bezel, shown three-quarter front, cropped at the bottom by the footer band. Its screen shows the Media Tribune Upcoming Events website: a royal blue header bar (about #2B4FB8) with the white serif wordmark 'MEDIA TRIBUNE' separated by a vertical rule from 'UPCOMING EVENTS' in small white capitals, and a white hamburger icon at right. Below it, 'Featured and Popular Events' in dark grey sans-serif with a teal 'ADD AN EVENT' pill button at right. Three event cards: a dark card with the Wonderfront DAY festival poster (white stylised 'DAY' lettering on a dark crowd photo) with a 'PREMIERE' label in teal, the title 'Wonderfront' and date and venue lines; a card with a concert photo under 'Fri 3 Jan 7:00 PM' and 'Battle of the Bands: Rock n' Roll' at Sunny's Tavern; a card with a green-lit science show photo under 'Sat 5 Jan' and 'Solids, Liquids, and Gases Science Show' at Hall of Science. Below the cards are the filter tabs 'Today', 'Tomorrow', 'Friday', 'Saturday', 'This weekend' (underlined in blue), 'Select categories', a dropdown reading 'Boston' and '20 Mi', a search field reading 'What are you looking for?', a button 'In-Person', the heading 'January 1, 2024', and a row of four event thumbnails cut off at the screen edge. Phone mockup (x 84.7, y 34.6, w 14.0, h 47.9 percent; screen 71 by 135 pixels for the device and 219 by 477 pixels for the screen), a black-bezelled smartphone overlapping the laptop's right edge. Its screen shows a Facebook feed: header 'facebook', a sponsored post from Events.com with the text 'Wonderfront Music Festival...', an image of a crowd with raised hands under palm trees with the white 'WONDERFRONT DAY' lettering and a centred play button, the caption 'Music Lovers Unite for Wonderfront' with a blue 'Buy Now' button, reaction counts '93.5K', '2.6K comments', '5.8K shares', '23.3K views', and Like, Comment, Send, Share actions. Lighting on the mockups is soft and even with faint grey drop shadows. Dominant colours are royal blue, white, black and cyan. The mockups are not cropped except the laptop base at the footer. A soft shadow image (x 50.5, y 21.0, w 47.6, h 64.1 percent) sits behind the laptop. Decorative thin grey-blue concentric arcs run across the upper right background. A thin blue rule (x 5.0, y 31.6, w 42.8, h 0.5 percent, 308 by 2 pixels) with a fade at its centre runs behind the 'Key Highlights' heading.

**Design**

Background is a vertical gradient from pale cyan #BDF4FF at the top to white #FFFFFF by about the middle of the slide, with faint concentric arc lines in light grey-blue #C9D6E3 across the upper right. Title text 'Events.com Ad Exchange' is Poppins Bold in near-black #1D1F20 at about 5.2 percent of slide height, and 'Media Network & Calendar' is Poppins Regular in the same colour and size. The subtitle is Poppins Bold in navy #0C3891 at about 3.5 percent. 'Key Highlights' is Poppins Bold in navy #0C3891 at about 2.5 percent. Bullets and body text are Poppins Regular in navy #0C3891 at about 2.5 percent, with bullet dots in the same navy. Accents are cyan #00BCDA for the footer band and corner circle, and the rule under the heading is a navy-to-white gradient line. The footer wordmark 'events.com' is a bold white rounded sans-serif and 'Create' is Poppins Regular in white. Shapes are flat with no corner radii, and the corner circle is a solid cyan disc with no shadow. Mockups carry soft grey drop shadows and no gradients beyond their photographic content.
