### Slide 26

**Review notes**

- Review: Many mid-deck slides are small-type and hard to read: slides 5 and 31 have tiny captions, slides 8 and 40 have very small text, slides 18 and 22 have barely legible bubble labels, and slides 23 to 27 repeat a template with small bullets over low-contrast screenshots.

**Text**

Events.com Execute / Helping organizers save time / Key Highlights / ● Check-in attendees and manage your event’s access / points with a seamless mobile check-in app / ● Rent tablets, chargers, backup batteries and hotspots / ● Enable fast entry using QR codes or voice-recognition / technology / ● Get real-time reporting on check-in stats / ● Sell tickets, registrations, and merchandise on-site with / our Point of Sale Kit, which includes tablets, card / readers, charging cables, tablet stands, receipt / printers, and more / events.com Create / 26

**Layout**

Single-column text on the left with a full-width device mockup group on the right. The title Events.com Execute sits top-left at about 3.4% from the top with a 2% left margin. The subtitle Helping organizers save time is centred near the top at x 35.5 to 64.6%. A section heading Key Highlights is centred over a horizontal rule at y 26.2%, and four bullets run left-aligned from x 6.5% down to y 85%. A full-width cyan footer bar sits at the bottom (y 94 to 100%) holding the wordmark on the left and the page number 26 at the far right.

**Visuals**

Two devices and a phone, all photographic mockup renders on a light background with soft drop shadows. (1) A dark grey tablet in landscape-to-portrait orientation at about x 62 to 93%, y 13 to 87%, with a black bezel and rounded corners. Its screen shows a white e-commerce checkout UI: top bar with back arrow and STEP 2. Complete Form, User: Max Garcia, and a step indicator of three dots on a line at top right. The left panel is titled OTHER STUFF and lists three products: T-Shirt (Unisex) $25.00 USD in White with quantity stepper showing 1 and an ADD TO CART button; Water Bottle $22.00 USD in White with quantity stepper showing 0 and a faded ADD TO CART button; Beanie $15.00 USD. The right panel shows Total $170.50 in green, a Purchase Summary with 1 DAY - GA $139.00, 1 T-shirt $35.00, Water Bottle (no price), then Subtotal, Fees, Taxes and Total rows with no values; a CANCEL ORDER link and a cyan NEXT button at bottom right, partly hidden by the phone. (2) A dark charcoal card reader with a rounded top and a circular lock icon in its face, sitting on top edge of the tablet at about x 79 to 88%, y 9 to 25%. (3) A smartphone in a silver frame at about x 84 to 94%, y 47 to 82%, showing a royal blue screen with two darker blue semicircles at top and bottom, the words Where events begin in white, a white-bordered black QR code, the text (1) and EVENT TICKET beneath it in white. Background decoration: thin white and pale grey concentric arcs radiating from the top-left area across the right half of the slide, faint and partly behind the devices. A solid cyan circle at top right.

**Design**

Background is a soft vertical gradient from light cyan at the top (about #BDF3FF) fading to near-white (#F7FCFF) at the bottom, with faint white concentric arc lines on it. Title Events.com Execute is #1D1F20 near-black, with Events.com in Poppins Bold and Execute in Poppins Regular, roughly 5.2% of slide height. Subtitle and Key Highlights heading are Poppins Bold in dark blue #0C3891 at about 3.5% and 2.5% of height. Bullets and body are Poppins Regular in #0C3891 at about 2.5% of height, with generous line spacing (about 1.6 times the font size within a wrapped bullet) and about 4% gaps between bullets. Bullet glyphs are small solid navy dots in a Times-style face. Accent colour is cyan #00BCDA used for the footer bar, the top-right circle and the tablet's cyan buttons. The heading rule is a thin horizontal line with navy ends fading to white in the middle. Footer wordmark events.com is bold white with Create in regular white. No corner radii on the slide itself; the devices have rounded corners and soft shadows.

### Slide 27

**Review notes**

- Review: Many mid-deck slides are small-type and hard to read: slides 5 and 31 have tiny captions, slides 8 and 40 have very small text, slides 18 and 22 have barely legible bubble labels, and slides 23 to 27 repeat a template with small bullets over low-contrast screenshots.

**Text**

Events.com Insights
All the data you need at your fingertips
Key Highlights
● Generate custom reports and access valuable / insights to make better, faster decisions without / spending money on additional analytics tools
● Access real-time data for your event sales
● Detailed analytics and marketing reports for / calendar listings and advertising campaigns / within the same platform you’re using for your / event listings
● Monitor your affiliate program efforts and track / comprehensive promo codes in real time
events.com Create
27

**Layout**

Two-column composition on a 16:9 slide. The title 'Events.com Insights' sits top-left at about 3.4% from the top with a 2% left margin. The centred subtitle sits at about 14% from the top. The 'Key Highlights' heading is centred over a horizontal divider on the left column, which starts at 5% and holds four bullets left-aligned from 6.5% (markers) and 9.7% (text). The right column holds a laptop mockup and a dashboard screenshot overlapping it, bleeding to the right edge area. A full-width cyan footer band runs along the bottom 6% with the wordmark left and page number 27 at the far right.

**Visuals**

Photograph-style product mockup, not a chart or table. Subject: a silver laptop (open, viewed from front, slightly angled, base at the bottom right) whose screen shows the events.com Data dashboard, with a dark left navigation sidebar holding icons labelled HOME, EVENT DETAILS, PAYMENT INFO, PROMOTIONS, DATA (highlighted), GIVING, PEOPLE and LANGUAGES. The screen shows the header 'Data', tabs Overview, Event Summary, Purchase Summary, a filter bar 'Filter By: Registration / Date Entire Event / Time Zone', 'Stats for Nov 9, 2019 - Nov 10, 2019', a 'Gender' donut chart with segments Male (light cyan), Female (salmon) and Unknown (grey) with legend, a 'Registration Summary' bar chart with light blue/cyan bars, and a 'Location' heading in the bottom. Overlapping the upper right of the laptop screen is a tall flat screenshot of the same dashboard (Fern Basin 5K event) showing Overall stat cards Active Registrations 407 and Net Revenue $10,666.25, circular metrics Registrations $10,666.25, Purchases $0.00, Discounts $-2,067.75, Refunds/Transfers $0.00, and a 'Registrations Over Time' line chart with blue area peaks, and 'Registration Summary' bar chart below. Lighting is soft and even, a neutral studio look. Dominant colours: silver grey, white, dark slate sidebar #3A4650, cyan #00BCDA accents, salmon #F08A6E. Not cropped; the laptop and screenshot are placed as cut-out images with a soft drop shadow over a light background. Decorative thin arcs run behind them.

**Design**

Background: Vertical gradient from pale cyan #BDF5FF at the top to white #FFFFFF near the bottom, with faint grey concentric arc lines. Light, airy, tech-style look. Background is a cyan-to-white vertical gradient with faint thin concentric grey arcs. Title 'Events.com' in bold near-black #1D1F20 and 'Insights' regular in the same colour. Subtitle and Key Highlights heading in bold dark blue #0C3891. Body bullets in regular dark blue #0C3891. Accent: cyan #00BCDA circle top right and footer band. Typeface: Poppins (geometric sans-serif), bold for headings and wordmark, regular for body. Title text about 5.2% of slide height (cap height about 3.6%), subtitle about 3.5%, Key Highlights about 2.5%, bullets about 2.5%, footer 'Create' about 2.7%, page number about 1.5%. Divider line is a 0.7% tall gradient bar that fades from dark blue #0C3891 at the ends to white in the middle. Bullet markers are small solid dark blue dots. No corner radii on text blocks; the decorative circle is a solid disc. Shadows only under the mockup images.

**Layout**

Two-column split slide. A solid royal-blue panel fills the left 40.3% of the width, full height, holding the title block at top, the bullet list in the vertical middle and the wordmark at bottom left. The right area is white with a pale cyan gradient, holding a centred intro paragraph at top, a laptop mockup in the centre, and a centred two-line caption below. Title sits top-left at about 8.7% from top with a 2% left margin. The page number is bottom right at about 96% from top. A cyan circle sits in the top-right corner.

**Visuals**

Laptop mockup, centred in the right area, spanning about x 48.6% to 91.8% and y 25.7% to 82.6%. Thin silver base and a black bezel frame the screen, with a soft grey drop shadow under the base. The screen shows the events.com homepage: a light grey top bar with the events.com logo at left and the links My Tickets, Discover Events, Create Events, Log In and a grid icon at right. Below is a blue hero area with the headline Find your next event. in large white sans-serif type, followed by a small white line reading Events near you. More than 186 million events based on your interests. Worldwide. A pale grey rounded search field reads Where? / New York with a cyan circular arrow button at its right end. Below that a small cyan line reads Log in / Sign Up to find events based on your interests. A circular photo crop of a group of smiling young people viewed from above, in sunglasses and casual clothes, sits at the right of the hero, overlapping the screen edge. Decorative cyan and lime-yellow circles and faint concentric arc lines sit on the blue hero. The screen bottom shows a white band with the label Trending cities in dark text. No photo or illustration outside the mockup. Only the laptop screen is a visual; the cyan circle top right is a filled shape.

Background is white fading to pale cyan toward the lower right, with faint white concentric arc lines on the right side. Left panel is solid royal blue #0C3891. Intro paragraph is regular Poppins in royal blue #0C3891 about 2.6%. Bullets are regular white Poppins about 3.9%, with white small round bullet dots. Caption is regular Poppins in near-black #1D1F20 about 2.2%. Page number is Poppins in cyan #00BCDA about 1.5%. Accent colour is cyan #00BCDA for the circle and page number. Sans-serif throughout, no shadows on text, rounded search field, circular photo crop.

### Slide 29

**Text**

Events.com Discover Is Key To Our Flywheel Opportunity / No one truly “owns” the event consumer / Events.com has the opportunity to become the home for the event world / More Event Creators / More Events / Event creators know they can connect to / If you’re a hotel, you’re on hotels.com…if / their audience on Events.com / you’re an event, you’re on events.com / More Event Goers / Events.com - first to mind for consumers to find events / events.com Discover / 29

**Layout**

Three-part composition on a centred vertical axis: a large circular flywheel sits in the middle of the slide with a headline line at the top and a centred caption at the bottom. Title sits top-left at about 2% left margin and 3.4% from top, left-aligned, spanning about 84% of the width. Two statements are centred above the flywheel, three labels with descriptive text are arranged left (right-aligned), right (left-aligned) and below (centred). A full-width navy footer bar occupies the bottom 6% with the wordmark at bottom left and the slide number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Circular flywheel diagram of three coloured arc arrows forming a ring around a central circular photograph. Top-left arc is dark navy #0C3891 with a navy arrowhead pointing right at the top; right arc is bright cyan #00BCDA with a cyan arrowhead pointing down at lower right; bottom arc is pale aqua #A6E9F2 with an arrowhead pointing up at lower left. Each arc has a soft grey drop shadow beneath (black at about 11% opacity). Three labelled callouts connect to the ring by thin straight leader lines ending in filled dots: navy dot and line to 'More Event Creators' at left, cyan dot and line to 'More Events' at right, pale aqua dot and vertical line to 'More Event Goers' at bottom. Central photograph: a smiling young woman with curly dark-auburn hair wearing light-blue over-ear headphones and a light mint-blue knit top, holding a black smartphone in both hands, with a gold wristwatch and a tan/orange backpack strap visible; she looks up and to the left against a dark grey blurred urban wall. Lighting is soft daylight, cool-toned. The photo is circular-cropped, sits inside a white ring with a light grey shadow edge, and overlaps the top navy and right cyan arcs. Background decoration: thin white concentric arc outlines radiating from the upper-right corner over the light background, and a solid cyan circle at top right.

**Design**

Background is a vertical gradient from pale sky blue #C4F3FF at the top to white #FFFFFF by about 45% of the height. Title text is near-black #1D1F20 in Poppins Bold, about 5.2% of slide height, sentence-case capitals on each word, no letter-spacing change. Statement lines are deep navy #0C3891 in Poppins Bold, about 3.5% of slide height. Callout headings: 'More Event Creators' navy #0C3891, 'More Events' cyan #00BCDA, 'More Event Goers' light cyan #54D5E6, all Poppins Bold about 3.0% of height. Body text is near-black #202124 in Poppins Bold, about 2.5% of height. Footer text is white Poppins, with 'events.com' in bold. Shapes have smooth rounded arc edges and no corner radii; arrowheads are sharp triangles. Shadows are soft and subtle, roughly 11% black. No borders on arcs; leader lines are thin, about 1 to 2 px, in matching accent colour.

### Slide 30

**Text**

Utilizing The Rapidly Evolving AI Landscape / The Events.com platform’s Intuitive User Interface and User Experience enables the seamless search for / events based on one’s interest, past behavior and location, with 100+ event categories to explore / AMERICAN FOOTBALL / SLOW FOOD / HAPPY HOUR / ACOUSTIC / Your Interests / Events.com’s AI engine learns from / each user’s individual interests, / demographic, behaviors and locations / 60M+ Annual Visitors(1) / 200M+ Experiences(1) / 7K Cities(1) / Our Events / Then the AI analyzes the data collected / and identifies the best matches with / events for every occasion / UTILIZING AI & ML TO LEARN YOUR SPECIFIC INTERESTS AND CONNECT YOU WITH THE BEST EVENTS(2) / (1) Source: Company data / (2) V2 planned to launch out of stealth in 2025 / events.com Discover / 30

**Layout**

Title sits top-left at about x 2%, y 3.4% on a pale sky background, with a teal circle at the top-right corner. Below the title, a centred two-line blue subtitle spans the slide. The middle section is three columns: left photo circle with tag cluster and centred caption, centre chip illustration, right map circle with stat callouts and centred caption. A full-width navy band at y 81.7% carries the white statement line, followed by a white footnote strip and a navy footer band at y 94% holding the wordmark at left and the page number at bottom-right, about x 97.9%.

**Visuals**

Five visuals. (a) Circular photograph at x 8.8%, y 32%, 15% wide by 26.7% high: a young woman with long light-brown hair, orange-tinted tinted sunglasses and an open-mouthed expression, seen in profile facing left, laughing or singing in a crowd; warm golden sunlight with lens flare, dominant colours amber, orange and skin tones; head-and-shoulders crop, circle-masked. Overlaid on its upper-right are four label tags stacked diagonally: AMERICAN FOOTBALL in yellow-gold text on white, SLOW FOOD in green text on white, HAPPY HOUR in pink-magenta text on white, and ACOUSTIC in red-orange text on white; several blurred translucent white ghost tiles sit beside them. (b) Centre illustration at x 38.1%, y 28.2%, 27.7% by 42.4%: a blue microchip (#2A5BC4-ish fill, dark-blue outline) with a rounded square body labelled events.com in white bold type under a teal (#00BCDA) filled circle; pins and circuit traces radiate from all four sides, ending in open circles and filled dots in blue (#2A5BC4) with 2px-style traces. (c) Circular map at x 74.2%, y 31.9%, 15% by 26.7%: a street map of New York area (labels such as Weehawken, New York, Jersey City, Brooklyn Heights) in pale grey, green and blue tones with many coloured pin markers (red, magenta, green, orange, teal) carrying music, food and ticket icons; circle-masked. (d) Teal elbow connector (#00BCDA) at x 71%, y 31%, 3.9% by 16.9% running from the 60M+ callout down to the 7K Cities callout with an arrowhead pointing right at Cities and another arrowhead pointing down into the 200M+ callout. (e) Decorative white concentric arc lines at the right side of the background at x 45.4%, y 0%, 54.6% by 96.9%, thin and low-contrast.

**Design**

Background: pale sky-blue gradient at top (#BFF1FF-ish) fading to near white through the middle, with thin white concentric arcs on the right. Title colour near-black #1D1F20; subtitle and section headings navy #0C3891; stat numbers and teal accents #00BCDA; caption body text dark grey #202124; statement band navy #0C3891 with white bold caps; footer band navy #0C3891. Typeface: Poppins throughout, geometric sans-serif; title Poppins Bold about 5.2% of slide height (cap height about 3.6%); subtitle Poppins Bold about 3% of slide height; section headings Poppins Bold about 2.6%; body text Poppins Light about 2% of slide height; footnotes Poppins Light about 1.25%. Callout boxes are white rounded rectangles with soft drop shadows; footnote strip is plain white.

### Slide 31

**Review notes**

- Review: Many mid-deck slides are small-type and hard to read: slides 5 and 31 have tiny captions, slides 8 and 40 have very small text, slides 18 and 22 have barely legible bubble labels, and slides 23 to 27 repeat a template with small bullets over low-contrast screenshots.

**Text**

Aggregating A Robust Data Set of Experiences / Globally distributed, diverse interest matrix with millions of users & billions of data points / Values / Needs / Big 5 / Openness (100%) / Openness to change (100%) / Conservation (89%) / Openness to change (5%) / Self-enhancement (82%) / Hedonism (46%) / Self-transcendence (5%) / Adventurousness (20%) / Artistic interests (8%) / Emotionality (10%) / Imagination (100%) / Intellect (99%) / Authority-challenging (99%) / Achievement striving (93%) / Cautiousness (88%) / Dutifulness (3%) / Orderliness (1%) / Self-discipline (85%) / Self-efficacy (11%) / Activity level (10%) / Assertiveness (10%) / Cheerfulness (10%) / Excitement-seeking (14%) / Outgoing (14%) / Gregariousness (14%) / Altruism (10%) / Cooperation (10%) / Modesty (10%) / Uncompromising (10%) / Simplicity (12%) / Trust (12%) / Fiery (5%) / Worry (6%) / Melancholy (9%) / Immoderation (16%) / Self-consciousness (7%) / Susceptible to stress (4%) / Challenge (13%) / Closeness (48%) / Curiosity (45%) / Excitement (76%) / Harmony (40%) / Ideal (41%) / Libery (49%) / Love (15%) / Practicality (75%) / Self-expression (86%) / Stability (20%) / Structure (43%) / Consciousness (90%) / Extraversion (10%) / Agreeableness (14%) / Emotional range (10%) / Data From Over(1): / 50K+ / Event Creators / 60M+ / Event Goers / 200M+ / Experiences / 128 / Countries / (2) / FORBES > INNOVATION / AI Needs Data More Than Data Needs AI / Rohit Sehgal Former Forbes Councils Member / Forbes Technology Council / COUNCIL POST | Membership (Fee-Based) / (1) Data screen from Pick1 in partnership with IBM Watson, which was acquired by Evensi, which was acquired by Events.com / (2) Forbes, October 5, 2023 / events.com Discover / 31

**Layout**

Full-bleed light slide with title at top left, one-line subtitle centred beneath it, and a large sunburst chart occupying the left half. The right half holds a row of four stat blocks under a Data From Over label and a Forbes article card below them. Footnotes sit bottom left above a full-width navy footer bar carrying the logo wordmark at left and the page number 31 at right; margins are about 2 percent.

**Visuals**

A Big Five sunburst chart (about 34 percent width, 65 percent height, left side) with three rings. Inner ring: Values, Needs, Big 5, Openness to change (100%, pink-tinted), Love (15%), Openness (100%), Conscientiousness (90%), Extraversion (10%), Agreeableness (14%), Emotional range (10%). Middle ring: Openness segments in mid blue, green Needs segments and red-pink Openness-to-change segments. Outer ring: each facet labelled with its percentage as listed in the text. Segment colours: blue #5AC8F5 for Big 5 traits, green #22B14C for Needs, pink #F4A6A6 for Values. A circular photo of a female athlete in a white sleeveless top and blue-tinted sunglasses, holding a water bottle, cropped to a circle with a navy #0C3891 ring, sits at the centre of the sunburst; the photo is outdoors with soft daylight and a blue-grey background. Right side: a screenshot of a Forbes article card with a white background, thin grey border and soft shadow, showing a small Forbes logo (black circle with white wordmark) at left. No other photographs or logos.

**Design**

Background: Gradient from light cyan #BDEFFF at top to white #FFFFFF at bottom, with faint white concentric arc lines on the right. Light sky-blue gradient background fading from cyan #BDEFFF at the top to white #FFFFFF at the bottom, with faint white concentric arc lines across the right half. Title in near-black #1D1F20; subtitle, Data From Over, stat numbers and footnote marker in royal navy #0C3891; stat labels in near-black #1D1F20; footer text in white on navy #0C3891. Typeface Poppins throughout: Poppins Bold for title, subtitle and stats, Poppins Light for footnotes, Poppins Regular for footer wordmark accents. Title about 5.2 percent of slide height, subtitle about 3.5 percent, stat numbers about 5 percent, stat labels about 2.6 percent, footnotes about 1.2 percent. Accent teal circle #00BCDA at top right with no outline. Article card has a thin light-grey border and soft drop shadow.

events.com
32

**Layout**

Single 16:9 slide with the title top-left at the left margin. A centred two-line subtitle sits below it, then a row of three navy phrases joined by two arrows, then a central events.com circle over a fan-shaped timeline with nine columns (year, logo, italic label) spread across the lower half. A full-width cyan footer bar sits at the bottom with the events.com wordmark at left and the page number at right; the footnote block sits just above the bar at left.

**Visuals**

Diagram and logos. Centre: a solid cyan (#00BCDA) circle reading 'events.com' in white bold sans-serif, sitting at the apex of a light-cyan triangle outline whose fill fades from cyan at the apex to transparent at the baseline. The baseline is a horizontal cyan line with nine short vertical cyan ticks rising from it, each above one year label. Two solid navy (#0C3891) right-pointing block arrows sit between the three phrases 'Platform Expansion', 'Client Growth' and 'Increase “ARPU”'. Nine logos, left to right: (1) EVENTS ONLINE.CA logo, red and blue with a blue figure; (2) BIB NUMBERS.com logo, blue tag and dark grey text; (3) spinGO logo, dark grey with lime-green GO; (4) evensi logo, red with a red icon; (5) helpget sponsors logo, grey and green; (6) Brown Paper Tickets round orange badge in white; (7) Wonderfront Music and Arts Festival round badge, teal and orange; (8) Balloon & Wine Festival badge, black text on white with a small 'Temecula Valley' line; (9) CITIFYD logo, orange C mark above orange wordmark. Background decoration: thin white concentric arc lines on the right half.

**Design**

Background is a light-cyan to white vertical gradient (top #BFF3FF fading to white near mid-slide) with faint white concentric arcs at the right. Title is near-black #1D1F20 Poppins Bold, approx 5.2% of slide height. Subtitle and phrases are navy #0C3891 Poppins Bold, about 3.5% and 3.0% of slide height. Year labels are #1D1F20 Poppins Bold about 2.6%. Column labels are #1D1F20 Poppins Italic about 2.2%. Footnotes are Poppins Light about 1.25%, black. Accents: cyan #00BCDA for the circle, triangle outline, ticks, top-right dot and footer bar. Shapes are flat with no shadows; the cyan outlines are about 2 px thin; arrows are solid navy blocks with no rounding.

### Slide 33

**Text**

Market Overview

**Layout**

A single centred title sits in the lower-middle of the slide, roughly 70% of the way down, horizontally centred (box x 30.6 to 69.5%). The photograph fills the whole slide behind it, with no grid, footer, slide number or logo.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed photograph of young children playing football on a grass lawn in a park. A boy in a dark Chelsea FC shirt with the club crest on the chest is running with the ball at centre; a younger boy in a dark top stands at left with his arms out; other children are blurred at left, top centre and right; a football is partly visible at the bottom centre. The camera is at low eye level, slightly shallow focus, and the subjects fill the frame. The photo is duotoned in cyan: the image is desaturated and tinted cyan-blue, with light areas near #A8E4F0 and shadows near #2A7F95. Overlaid on the photo are five thin concentric white circle outlines centred to the right of the frame, around the teal disc, at about 40% opacity, plus a solid teal filled circle at upper right (x 80.5%, y 15.6%, w 10.7%, h 19.0%, a 213 by 213 pixel placed element) in #22BEE0.

**Design**

Background: Cyan-tinted photograph of children playing football, full-bleed over a white #FFFFFF base. Full-bleed cyan-tinted photograph over a white base. Title in heavy geometric sans-serif (Poppins Bold), near-black #1D1F20, single line, about 7.9% of slide height (cap height about 5.5% of slide height). Accent teal #22BEE0 for the disc; white #FFFFFF for the ring lines. No shadows, no corner radii, no gradients beyond the photo's natural tonal range.

### Slide 34

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Uses large paired numbers for 2024 and 2029 market size with a clean two-column layout that makes the growth comparison readable at a glance.
- Review: Slide 34 charts are clean but the bars are unevenly scaled and the layout is busy, so the strongest content is still diluted by density and uneven polish.

**Text**

The Global Experience Economy is Massive / Events.com connects and powers the global experience ecosystem / Large and Growing Market / Key Trends / Global Event Management is / a Large Opportunity (1) / $936B / $1.8T / CAGR: 11% / 2024E / 2029E / $95B (3) / 2023 US Live / Entertainment Spending / 74% (4) / Of Consumers Are Likely / to Buy Based on / Experiences Alone / 23% (5) / Increase in Live Event / Spending in 2023 / Event Management Software / is a High Growth Industry (2) / $16B / $35B / CAGR: 17% / 2024E / 2029E / (1) Arizton, March 2024 / (2) Markets and Markets, April 2024 / (3) U.S. Bureau of Economic Analysis / (4) Contentserv / (5) Marketplace, October 19, 2023 / events.com / 34

**Layout**

Two-column body on a 2-column grid below a centred subtitle: the left column (about x 2 to 50) holds a 'Large and Growing Market' header bar, two gradient chevrons with bar charts; the right column (about x 51 to 98) holds a 'Key Trends' header bar, a photo-circle stack with a connector arrow and stat blocks. The title sits top-left at about y 3 to 9, the subtitle is centred at about y 14, slide number is bottom-right inside a full-width cyan footer band (y 94 to 100) that also carries the events.com wordmark bottom-left; footnotes sit bottom-left above the footer. Left margin about 2%, right margin about 2%.

**Visuals**

Two grouped bar charts on the left, each a pair of vertical bars with a diagonal growth arrow. Chart 1 (top): bars for 2024E valued $936B (cyan #00BCDA, about 9% of slide height tall) and 2029E valued $1.8T (navy #0C3891, taller); a cyan diagonal arrow with label 'CAGR: 11%' rotated about 30 degrees rises across the top of the bars; category labels 2024E and 2029E sit under a thin light-grey baseline (about #D9D9D9). Chart 2 (bottom): 2024E $16B (cyan, short) and 2029E $35B (navy, taller) with arrow labelled 'CAGR: 17%' and the same baseline and category labels. No explicit axis, units are in the value labels. Right column: a circular photo (top) of a female singer in a red sequined outfit holding a microphone on a dark blue stage with stage light, cropped as a circle, full colour, dominant blue and red-orange. Below it an L-shaped cyan connector: a vertical line down from the photo with an arrowhead pointing down to a second circular photo, and a horizontal branch with arrowhead pointing right toward the 74% stat. Bottom circular photo: a cheering crowd at a sports event, a man with arms raised in a blue and white striped shirt at centre, people around him, warm dark brown and blue tones with low light. Third circular photo (right, overlapping the stat area): a group of about eight smiling, laughing people posing for a selfie outdoors, bright daylight, shallow focus, dominant skin tones with blue and brown clothing, boxed as a circle with no border. Two chevron-shaped gradient banners on the left, white on the left fading to cyan #00BCDA on the right, pointed right, behind the first two text lines.

**Design**

Background is a pale vertical gradient from light cyan #BFF0FF at the top to near white #F7FCFF at the middle and bottom, with faint thin white concentric arc lines in the upper right. Title text is near-black #1D1F20 in Poppins Bold, about 5.2% of slide height for the cap-height line. Subtitle is navy #0C3891 Poppins Bold about 3.5%. Body text is #1D1F20 Poppins Regular about 2.2%. Stat numbers are navy #0C3891 Poppins Bold about 4.9%. Header bars are solid navy #0C3891 rectangles with white Poppins Bold text about 2.5%. Accent colour is cyan #00BCDA for bars, arrows, decorative circle and footer band. Footnotes are Poppins Light about 1.25% black #000000. Shapes have sharp corners, no shadows, no outlines; photos are circular with no border.

### Slide 35

**Text**

Events.com Market Position / Large Arena / Events / Pro / Big Name / Sports / Concerts / SERVING THE EXPANSIVE MID-MARKET & EXPANDING / Festivals / Charity / Sport / Food / & Concerts / & Causes / & Endurance / & Drinks / Art / Business / Wellness / Nightlife / & Leisure / & Technology / & Health / & Theatre / SMALL PRIVATE GET TOGETHERS / WEDDING SHOWERS / HOLIDAY PARTIES / events.com / 35

**Layout**

Single slide with a title at top left, a pyramid-style graphic in the centre and a cyan footer bar along the bottom. The central cyan-tinted panel spans about 10% to 90% of the width and holds a 4 by 2 grid of icon circles with labels, arranged in two columns of pairs either side of a central double-headed arrow. The base trapezoid's labels sit in a row across the bottom, and the page number sits at bottom right in the footer.

**Visuals**

Icon set of eight white line icons in solid cyan circles. Left column top to bottom: music notes (Festivals & Concerts), palette and paintbrush (Art & Leisure). Second column: hand holding a heart (Charity & Causes), briefcase (Business & Technology). Third column, right of the arrow: bicycle (Sport & Endurance), heartbeat in heart outline (Wellness & Health). Right column: beer mugs (Food & Drinks), theatre comedy and tragedy masks (Nightlife & Theatre). A navy double-headed vertical arrow sits between the two groups, about 49.5% from left, spanning about 52% to 88% of height. A thin navy single-headed arrow points up from the panel's top edge at about 49.8% from left, between about 34% and 39% of height. Navy outline triangle (apex at about 50%, 7.7%) forms the pyramid behind the panel, with the labels Large Arena Events at the apex, Pro Sports and Big Name Concerts beside the upper sides. A navy outline trapezoid forms the base, running from about 3.5%, 90.5% to 96.3%, 89.8% at its bottom edge, with its labels inside. Faint white concentric arcs run across the upper right of the background.

**Design**

Background: Vertical gradient from light cyan #BFF5FF at top to white #FFFFFF around mid-height, with faint white concentric ring arcs at upper right. Light cyan-to-white vertical gradient background with thin white concentric ring arcs at the upper right. Title in bold geometric sans-serif (Poppins Bold), near-black #1D1F20, about 5.2% of slide height. Body labels in Poppins Regular, #1D1F20, about 2.5% of slide height. Panel heading in Poppins Bold, navy #05173D, about 3.0% of slide height. Icon labels in Poppins Bold, black #000000, about 2.2% of slide height. Accent colour cyan #00BCDA for footer bar, top-right circle and icon circles. Panel fill #C4F6FF with navy #05173D thin outline. Pyramid and base outlines navy #05173D, about 0.2% stroke width. Icon circles cyan #00BCDA at 85% opacity. No shadows or corner radii; the panel corners are square.

### Slide 36

**Text**

Levers for Growth
Events.com has a comprehensive strategy to expand revenue velocity and
customer engagement through a “buy, build, and partner” playbook
Expand Discover
City-by-City
AI-Driven
Personalization
New Revenue
Streams
Innovative
Marketing
Expand
Product Offerings
Consumer Data
Capitalization
Curve acceleration expected
from future acquisitions
events.com
36

**Layout**

Free-form diagonal composition on a 100 percent width slide. Title sits top-left at about x 2 and y 3.4, left-aligned, with a two-line subtitle beneath it at about y 13.7. A rising chain of six circular icon nodes runs diagonally from lower-left to upper-right along a thick arrow, each node with a leader line and a label. A boxed callout sits lower right, and a full-width cyan footer band at the bottom carries the wordmark at left and the page number at right.

**Visuals**

Diagram: a thick ascending arrow-shaped band with a gradient from light cyan (#00BCDA) at lower-left to deep navy blue (#0C3891) at upper-right, ending in a pointed arrowhead at top right. Six white circles with thin cyan (#00BCDA) outlines sit on the band, each holding a black line icon, from lower-left to upper-right: (1) a briefcase above a handshake, labelled Expand Discover City-by-City; (2) a handshake above a dollar coin with up-arrows, labelled Expand Product Offerings; (3) a microchip with circuit traces, labelled AI-Driven Personalization; (4) a storefront with striped awning, labelled Innovative Marketing; (5) three people beside a rising trend line with arrowhead, labelled New Revenue Streams; (6) a stack of black boxes, labelled Consumer Data Capitalization. Each circle has a thin cyan vertical leader line to its label; labels above the circles for nodes 1, 3, 5 and below for nodes 2, 4, 6. Background decoration: a set of concentric thin white-grey arcs centred off the upper right, drawn over the gradient, and a solid cyan filled circle at top right. Callout: rectangle with a 3 pt cyan (#00BCDA) stroke, no fill, square corners, containing the bold navy text Curve acceleration expected / from future acquisitions. Icons are black line or solid glyphs; no photographs.

**Design**

Background: Vertical gradient from light cyan (#BFF3FF) at top to white (#FFFFFF) in the middle, with concentric thin grey-white arcs overlaid. Pale sky-blue to white vertical gradient background, cyan at top (about #BFF3FF) fading to white (#FFFFFF) near the middle and lower area. Title in near-black #1D1F20, bold geometric sans-serif (Poppins Bold), about 5.2 percent of slide height. Subtitle and callout in navy #0C3891, Poppins Regular for subtitle at about 3.0 percent of slide height, with the phrase in quotes set in Poppins Bold. Node labels in near-black #1D1F20, Poppins Bold, about 2.2 percent. Callout text in navy #0C3891, Poppins Bold, about 3.5 percent. Accent colour cyan #00BCDA for circle outlines, leader lines, footer band, decorative circle and callout border. Leader lines are 2 px-equivalent solid cyan strokes. Circles are fully round with cyan rims. Footer band has no shadow.

### Slide 37

**Text**

Key Business Metrics

**Layout**

Centered composition on a full-bleed photo. A cyan circular logo sits at the horizontal centre near the upper middle of the slide, with the title centred horizontally below it at about 70% of the slide height. There is no footer, slide number, or other text. Margins are not applicable because the photo bleeds to every edge.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed photograph of a crowd at an outdoor event, tinted pale teal. A woman in the left foreground raises one arm with her eyes closed and smiles; she wears a sleeveless top with partly visible printed lettering reading SAINT-ÉMILION and MAIN TOUR. To the right and lower right, a man in a cap printed BORN & THIRSTY, with sunglasses, is partly visible. Bokeh light spots fill the background. Lighting is soft and diffuse, and the image is cropped at all edges. Over the photo are five concentric thin white semi-transparent rings centred on the logo, spanning the full slide. A solid cyan circle logo with a tiny ™ mark sits at its centre.

**Design**

Background is a teal-tinted, desaturated photograph with a pale cyan-to-teal gradient, lighter at the bottom-left and darker teal at the top-right. Title text is near-black #1D1F20 in Poppins Bold, with no accent colour on the text. The logo circle is cyan #00BCDC. Concentric rings are white at about 40% opacity with a thin stroke of about 0.3% of slide height. Title type is about 7.9% of slide height, heavy and geometric sans-serif. No shadows or corner radii.

### Slide 38

**Text**

How We Make Money
We help event creators save time and make more money through a diverse set of current and future offerings

### Slide 40

**Review notes**

- Review: Many mid-deck slides are small-type and hard to read: slides 5 and 31 have tiny captions, slides 8 and 40 have very small text, slides 18 and 22 have barely legible bubble labels, and slides 23 to 27 repeat a template with small bullets over low-contrast screenshots.
- Suggested repair: Move disclaimers and risk text to an appendix in readable type, tighten the story order so the company case comes first, and enlarge the small text and labels on slides 8, 16, 18, 22 and 40.

**Text**

Transaction Overview

**Layout**

Single centred title line set low on the slide over a full-bleed photograph, with a solid cyan circle centred above it and concentric rings behind both. The title's horizontal centre sits at about 50% of the slide width, with no footer, page number or logo visible.

**Visuals**

Photograph: an American football stadium seen from high in the stands, looking down onto the green field with yard lines, end-zone markings and small figures on the field. Crowds fill the stands on both sides, stadium light towers stand at the upper left and right, two scoreboards sit at upper left and upper right of the centre, and a sky with white cumulus clouds fills the top. Lighting is bright daylight. The photo is full-bleed, uncropped at the edges, and rendered as a cyan monotone duotone (light cyan to white) with a light cyan wash over the whole image. Dominant colours are cyan #00BCDC, pale cyan #8ED8E8 and white. Overlay: five thin, semi-transparent white concentric circles (about 40% opacity, about 0.3% of slide height stroke) centred on the solid circle, extending past the slide edges. Solid circle: flat cyan #00BCDC, no outline, no shadow, centred horizontally, sitting above the title.

**Design**

Background is a cyan-tinted stadium photograph with no gradient on its own, though the bottom of the photo fades slightly lighter. Title text is near-black #1D1F20 in Poppins Bold, sentence/title case, no letter-spacing change, about 7.9% of slide height. The accent is cyan #00BCDC used for the solid circle. Concentric ring lines are semi-transparent white with rounded circular geometry. No shadows, no corner radii, no borders.

### Slide 41

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Dense financial slide that organizes sources and uses, ownership and valuation into labeled panels with numbered footnotes for assumptions.
- Worth keeping: Keep the visual identity, the problem and solution pair on slides 11 and 12, the cover, and the clear numbered tables on slide 41.

**Text**

SPAC Transaction Overview
Transaction Highlights(1)
Valuation
• $399M pro forma enterprise value of combined company
• Implied pre-money market capitalization of $314M
• Excludes value of 4M earnout shares available to Events.com shareholders subject to share price targets
Financing
• Transaction expected to provide gross proceeds of up to $50M, through a PIPE or alternative financing and Cash in Trust after final redemptions
Deal Structure
• Events.com shareholders rolling 100% of their equity, expected to own ~72.4% of the combined entity
Pro Forma Valuation at Close(1)
PF Shares Outstanding (M) 43.4
Share Price ($) $10.00
PF Equity Value ($M) $434
(-) PF Net Cash ($M) ($35)
PF Enterprise Value ($M) $399
Sources and Uses(1)
Sources ($M) Uses ($M)
Events.com Rollover Equity $314 Equity to Events.com $314
Cash in Trust / PIPE $50 Cash to Balance Sheet $35
Transaction Expenses $15
Total $364 Total $364
Pro Forma Ownership at Close(1)
Shares (M) % Own.
1 Events.com Equity 31.4 72.4%
2 SPAC Sponsor 7.0 16.1%
3 SPAC Public Shareholders / Other 5.0 11.5%
events.com
41
(1) Assumptions: / Assumes no cash or debt on the balance sheet prior to the transaction / Excludes the impact of certain Events.com convertible securities on the capitalization of the combined company / 43.4M pro forma shares outstanding at $10.00 per common share / Assumes $50.0M from a combination of cash retained in trust post extension and financing raised; SPAC cash amount is subject to change depending on the actual interest earned in the trust. / All charts and tables exclude 5.4M sponsor warrants and 9.3M public warrants; All warrants have a strike price of $11.50 per common share / SPAC sponsor shares includes 1M sponsor shares to be “transferred” to Events.com at close / Excludes 4M earnout shares that will equally vest at each of the following price thresholds if the volume-weighted average price (“VWAP”) of the Surviving PubCo Common Stock exceeds $12.50, $15.00, $17.50, and $20.0 for at least 20 trading days during any 30 consecutive trading day period

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a full-bleed slide: left column holds Transaction Highlights above Pro Forma Valuation at Close; right column holds Sources and Uses above Pro Forma Ownership at Close with a donut chart at its lower right. The title sits top-left at about 3.4% from the top and 2% from the left edge; a footnote block spans the lower area above a full-width cyan footer bar carrying the events.com wordmark at left and the page number 41 at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Donut chart (Pro Forma Ownership at Close), no printed chart title beyond the section heading; three segments, no axes. Segment 1 Events.com Equity, dark blue #23559A, about 72.4%, largest segment, spanning from 12 o'clock clockwise around the right and bottom; marker circle labelled 1 sits on it at lower right. Segment 2 SPAC Sponsor, mid blue #3FAEEE, 16.1%, upper left; marker circle labelled 2 sits on it at top. Segment 3 SPAC Public Shareholders / Other, pale blue #E0F3FF, 11.5%, left; marker circle labelled 3 sits on it. Segments are separated by thin white gaps; donut hole is centre-filled with the background gradient. Values printed in the table beside it: 31.4M / 72.4%, 7.0M / 16.1%, 5.0M / 11.5%. Tables: Pro Forma Valuation at Close, 3 columns-free two-column table, 5 rows: PF Shares Outstanding (M) 43.4 (shaded #D0DDF5, bold); Share Price ($) $10.00 (white, regular); PF Equity Value ($M) $434 (shaded, bold); (-) PF Net Cash ($M) ($35) (white, regular); PF Enterprise Value ($M) $399 (shaded, bold). Sources and Uses: two side-by-side tables with header rows and a rule beneath headers. Sources table columns Sources and ($M): Events.com Rollover Equity $314; Cash in Trust / PIPE $50; Total $364 (shaded #D8EAFF, bold). Uses table columns Uses and ($M): Equity to Events.com $314; Cash to Balance Sheet $35; Transaction Expenses $15; Total $364 (shaded #D8EAFF, bold). Pro Forma Ownership table: columns Shares (M) and % Own.; rows 1 Events.com Equity 31.4 72.4%; 2 SPAC Sponsor 7.0 16.1%; 3 SPAC Public Shareholders / Other 5.0 11.5%. Decorative: thin concentric arcs in pale grey-white lines sweep from top centre to the right edge behind the right column; no photographs. Logo: events.com wordmark in white bold sans-serif at bottom left on the footer bar.

**Design**

Background is a vertical light cyan to white gradient, roughly #BEF3FF at the top fading to #FFFFFF by mid-slide, with faint grey-white concentric arc lines on the right. Title is Poppins Bold in near-black #1D1F20, about 5.2% of slide height (cap height about 3.6%). Section headings are Poppins Bold in dark royal blue #0C3891, about 2.5% of slide height, with a thin gradient rule (dark blue #0C3891 fading to pale on the outer sides) running at the same level, height about 0.5%. Body text is Poppins regular in black #000000 and #1D1F20, about 2.0% of slide height; sub-bullets indented. Bullet glyphs are Arial-style solid dots. Table header and total text is Poppins Bold #1D1F20; shaded table rows #D0DDF5, totals #D8EAFF; table rules are thin dark lines under header rows. Accents: cyan #00BCDA for the footer bar and top-right circle, blues #23559A, #3FAEEE, #E0F3FF in chart. Footnotes are Poppins Italic black at about 1.0% of slide height. Shapes have square corners; top-right decorative circle is a solid filled circle with no outline or shadow; chart has no shadow.

### Slide 42

**Text**

Risk Factors / All references to the “Company,” “Events.com,” “we”, “us” and “our” refer to Events.com, Inc. prior to the consummation of the Proposed Transaction, and the combined company following the consummation of the Proposed / Transaction. The risks presented below are certain of the general risks related to the Company’s business, industry and ownership structure and are not exhaustive. The list below is qualified in its entirety by disclosures contained / in future filings by the Company, or by third parties (including Concord Acquisition Corp II) with respect to the Company, with the SEC. These risks speak only as of the date of this Presentation and neither the Company nor / Concord makes any commitment to update such disclosures. The risks highlighted in future filings with the SEC may differ significantly from and will be more extensive than those presented below. / • We are an early-stage company with a history of losses, and expect to incur significant losses for the foreseeable future. / • We may not be able to achieve our expected business milestones or launch products on our anticipated timelines. / • The COVID-19 pandemic has had a material negative impact on our business and operating results and any re-occurrence or similar pandemics could materially affect our business. / • Our business is dependent on the continued occurrence of sporting events, concerts and theater shows and on relationships with buyers, sellers and distribution partners and any change in such occurrence or relationships / could adversely affect our business. / • Changes in Internet search engine algorithms or changes in marketplace rules could have a negative impact on traffic for our sites and ultimately, our business and results of operations. / • We face intense competition in the event space. / • If we do not continue to maintain and improve our platform and brand or develop successful new solutions and enhancements or improve existing ones, our business will suffer. / • We may be adversely affected by the occurrence of extraordinary events or factors affecting concert, sporting and theater events. / • We may be unsuccessful in potential future acquisition endeavors and we may not be successful in integrating past acquisitions into our business. / • The failure to retain, motivate or integrate any of our senior management team or other skilled personnel could have an adverse effect on our business, financial condition or results of operations. / • The processing, storage, use and disclosure of personal data could give rise to liabilities as a result of governmental regulation, conflicting legal requirements or applications of privacy regulations. / • Unfavorable legislative outcomes, or outcomes in legal proceedings in which we may be involved, may adversely affect our business and operating results. / • System interruption and the lack of integration and redundancy in our systems and infrastructure may have an adverse impact on our business, financial condition and results of operations. / • Cyber security risks, data loss or other breaches of our network security could materially harm our business and results of operations. / • We may fail to adequately protect or enforce our intellectual property rights or face potential liability and expense for legal claims alleging that the operation of our business infringes intellectual property rights of third / parties. / • Our payments system depends on third-party providers. / • The announcement of the Proposed Transaction could disrupt Events.com’s business. / • The dual class structure of our Common Stock has the effect of concentrating voting control with our co-founders. This will limit or preclude your ability to influence corporate matters, including the outcome of important / transactions, including a change in control. / • Directors and officers of Concord have potential conflicts of interest in recommending that stockholders vote in favor of approval of the Proposed Transaction. / • Concord stockholders will have a reduced ownership and voting interest after the Proposed Transaction and will exercise less influence over management. / • The ability of Concord stockholders to exercise redemption rights with respect to a large number of shares could deplete Concord’s trust account prior to the Proposed Transaction and thereby diminish the amount of / working capital of the combined company after the Proposed Transaction (the “Combined Company”). / • Concord’s initial stockholders, directors, officers, advisors, and their affiliates may purchase shares or public warrants from public stockholders, which may reduce the public “float” of Concord’s Class A common stock. / • The Proposed Transaction may not be completed by Concord’s business combination deadline in its organizational documents, as amended to date, and Concord may fail to obtain an extension of the business combination / deadline. / • There can be no assurance that the Proposed Transaction will achieve the Company’s objectives of providing the Company with sufficient capital, and if the Company requires additional capital to fund its operations or / expected growth, there can be no assurance that the Company will be able to obtain such funds on attractive terms or at all, and the Combined Company’s stockholders may experience dilution as a result. / • The Company and Concord have incurred and will incur substantial costs in connection with the Proposed Transaction and related transactions, such as legal, accounting, consulting, and financial advisory fees. / • While the Company and Concord work to complete the Proposed Transaction, management’s focus and resources may be diverted from operational matters and other strategic opportunities. / • If PIPE financing is not identified by the Company and Concord, or if identified, is consummated on different terms than those currently contemplated or fails to close and sufficient stockholders exercise their redemption / rights in connection with the Proposed Transaction, Concord may lack sufficient funds to consummate the Proposed Transaction. / • The Company’s operations may be restricted during the pendency of the Proposed Transaction pursuant to terms of the merger agreement for the Proposed Transaction. / • The announcement of the Proposed Transaction could disrupt the Company’s relationships with its customers, suppliers and others, as well as its operating results and business generally. / • Uncertainty about the effect of the Proposed Transaction may affect the Company’s ability to retain key employees and integrate management structures and may negatively impact its management, strategy and results of / operations. / events.com / 42

**Layout**

Single full-width text column on a light cyan gradient background with the title Risk Factors top-left at about 2% left margin, a body paragraph beneath, and a bullet list running down the slide to about 92% height. A full-width cyan footer band occupies the bottom 6% with the events.com wordmark at the left and the slide number 42 at the bottom right. Body text is left-aligned with a hanging indent under the bullets, and the paragraph lines run almost to the right margin at about 97.9%.

**Visuals**

Decorative line art of parallel and concentric thin arcs in pale grey-white strokes, occupying the upper right area from about x 45% to the right edge and from the top to about 97% height, drawn over the gradient and behind the text. A solid cyan circle sits at the top right corner. No photographs, charts or logos other than the events.com wordmark in the footer.

**Design**

Background is a vertical gradient from pale cyan #BDF3FC at the top to white #FFFFFF near the middle and bottom. Title colour is near-black #1D1F20, body text is mid-grey #7F7F7F, accent is cyan #00BCDA used for the circle and footer band, and the footer wordmark and page number are white #FFFFFF. Title is Poppins Bold, sized about 5.2% of slide height. Body text is Poppins Regular, about 1.5% of slide height, with bullets in Arial. The arcs are hairline grey-white strokes with no fill. Circle and footer band are flat with no corner radius, shadow or gradient.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

- illustration at x 45.4, y 0, w 54.6, h 96.9: Decorative line art of concentric and parallel thin arcs (Hairline strokes in pale grey-white, roughly 0.5 pt, no fill; the arcs cross behind the body text and the footer band clips them at the bottom; opacity low.)

### Slide 43

**Review notes**

- Review: The route is weakened by front-loaded and back-loaded legal text (slides 2 and 43 are tiny, unreadable walls of text), SPAC and presenter slides before the company is introduced, and a close on risk factors and a photo with no summary or next step.

**Text**

Risk Factors
•
The consummation of the Proposed Transaction is subject to a number of conditions and if those conditions are not satisfied or waived, the merger agreement for the Proposed Transaction may be terminated in accordance
with its terms and the Proposed Transaction may not be completed.
The Combined Company may incur successor liabilities due to conduct arising prior to the completion of the Proposed Transaction.
Subsequent to the completion of the Proposed Transaction, the Combined Company may be exposed to unknown or contingent liabilities and may be required to take write-downs or write-offs, restructuring and impairment
or other charges that could have a significant negative effect on its financial condition, results of operations and the price of its securities.
The obligations associated with being a public company will involve significant expenses and will require significant resources and management attention, which may divert from the Combined Company’s business
operations.
The Company’s management and current resources may not successfully or effectively manage the transition to a public company.
Future sales of common stock after the consummation of the Proposed Transaction may cause the market price of the Combined Company’s common stock to drop significantly, even if the Company’s business is doing well.
Following the Proposed Transaction, outstanding warrants will become exercisable for the Combined Company’s common stock, which would increase the number of shares eligible for future resale in the public market and
result in dilution to the Combined Company’s stockholders.
The Combined Company does not intend to pay cash dividends for the foreseeable future.
The Combined Company may not meet the NYSE American’s initial listing criteria, and even if it does, the NYSE American may not continue to list the Combined Company’s securities on its exchange, which could limit the
ability of investors in the Combined Company to make transactions in the Combined Company’s securities and subject the Combined Company to additional trading restrictions.
If the Proposed Transaction’s benefits do not meet the expectations of investors or securities analysts, the market price of Concord’s securities or, following the consummation of the Proposed Transaction, the Combined
Company’s securities, may decline.
If securities or industry analysts do not publish research, or publish inaccurate or unfavorable research, about the Combined Company’s business, the price and trading volume of its securities could decline.
There has been no prior public market for the Company’s securities. The stock price of the Combined Company’s common stock may be volatile or may decline regardless of its operating performance.
Legal proceedings may be instituted against the Proposed Transaction, which could delay or prevent or otherwise adversely impact the Proposed Transaction.
events.com
43

**Layout**

Single left-aligned text column under a top-left title, with the title at about 2% left margin and 3% from the top. The bullet list runs full width from about 2% to 97% of the slide, starting just below the title. A solid cyan footer band spans the bottom 6% of the slide, carrying the white events.com wordmark at left and the slide number 43 at the far right. The cyan circle sits in the top-right corner.

**Visuals**

Decorative background illustration: a set of thin, concentric white arc lines sweeping from the top edge down and to the right edge, drawn over a pale cyan gradient. The arcs are white at about 60% opacity on light cyan, and they run behind the text on the right half of the slide. Solid cyan circle, top right, flat with no outline. Solid cyan footer band across the full width at the bottom. Wordmark 'events.com' in white bold sans-serif in the footer. No photographs or charts.

**Design**

Background is a pale cyan to white vertical gradient, with white arc line decoration. Title is Poppins Bold in near-black #1D1F20, large at about 5.2% of slide height. Body text is Poppins Regular in mid-grey #7F7F7F at about 1.5% of slide height, with bullets in grey ArialMT. Accent colour is cyan #00BCDA for the footer band and the top-right circle. Body paragraphs are justified with line spacing of about 1.2 lines, and bullet items are separated by roughly 0.3% of slide height. Shapes are flat with no corner radius, shadow or outline. Wordmark and page number are white.

### Slide 44

**Text**

events.com

**Layout**

Full-bleed photograph fills the whole slide with no margins. A single wordmark sits left of centre in the upper third, roughly 10% from the left edge and about 17% from the top. There is no title, footer, slide number or other text.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed photograph of an outdoor hot-air balloon event seen from behind a crowd. In the foreground, three people stand close with arms around each other, seen from behind: a person in a red long-sleeved top with curly brown hair on the left, a person in a grey knit beanie in the centre, and a person with long straight brown hair on the right in a tan coat. The left figure raises one arm, and the right figure points upward with a raised hand. A boy in a red shirt stands at the far right edge with his arm outstretched. Behind them, a crowd of spectators holds up phones and watches. Large hot-air balloons fill the sky: a yellow, teal and orange checkered balloon at the top right, a multicoloured striped balloon in the centre, a blue striped balloon near the centre right, and many small balloons scattered across the pale blue sky. Lighting is bright, clear daylight with soft haze. Dominant colours are sky blue, red-orange, tan and yellow. The photo is uncropped and not boxed. A thin, semi-transparent white concentric ring pattern, centred near the middle of the slide at about x 36%, y 34%, overlays the whole photograph, with rings of various radii extending past the slide edges. The rings are about 1 pixel wide, white at roughly 40 to 60 percent opacity, and sit over the figures and sky without a tint. A small white rectangle is also present near x 44.7%, y 19.8%, 5.9% wide and 7.0% tall, placed over the photograph and to the right of the wordmark, with no visible content.

**Design**

Background: Photograph of a crowd watching hot-air balloons, full-bleed, behind white concentric ring lines. Photographic background with a white wordmark and thin translucent white concentric rings as the only graphic. Wordmark color is white #FFFFFF with a cyan #1EB4D6 full stop. The wordmark is a geometric sans-serif, medium to bold weight, lowercase, with no italics, capitals or letter-spacing change. Its size is about 12% of slide height, with ascender height about 9% of slide height. Body text: none. Rings have no fills, shadows or gradients, and are white at about 50% opacity with stroke width about 1 pixel. The photograph has no overlay tint.
